    "                    \"accessToken\": probe.get(\"access_token\"),\n"
    "                    \"fileIds\": file_ids,\n"
    "                    \"parentId\": parent_id,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                share_id = str(probe.get(\"share_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if share_id:\n"
    "                    restore_payload[\"shareId\"] = share_id\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【转存提交】share=%s 目录=%s 提交文件=%s\",\n"
    "                    share_id or \"-\",\n"
    "                    normalized,\n"
    "                    len(file_ids),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                response = client._request(\n"
    "                    method=\"POST\",\n"
    "                    url=f\"{client.API_BASE_URL}/nd.bizuserres.s/v1/restore_share\",\n"
    "                    data=restore_payload,\n"
    "                    need_auth=True,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if not self._is_success(response):\n"
    "                    message = str(response.get(\"msg\") or response.get(\"error\") or \"光鸭增量转存失败\")\n"
    "                    self._set_job_state(job_key, \"failed\", error=message, task_ids=task_ids)\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"success\": False,\n"
    "                        \"message\": message,\n"
    "                        \"completed_items\": completed,\n"
    "                        \"task_ids\": task_ids,\n"
    "                        \"retryable\": True,\n"
    "                        \"stage\": \"restore_share\",\n"
    "                        \"reason\": \"api_not_success\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                data = response.get(\"data\") or {}\n"
    "                task_id = str(data.get(\"taskId\") or data.get(\"task_id\") or \"\") if isinstance(data, dict) else \"\"\n"
    "                if task_id:\n"
    "                    task_ids.append(task_id)\n"
    "                self._set_job_state(job_key, \"submitted\", task_ids=task_ids, group_paths=group_paths)\n"
    "                if task_id and hasattr(api, \"_wait_task_done\"):\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【转存任务】等待增量任务完成：task_id=%s\", task_id)\n"
    "                    done = api._wait_task_done(task_id, max_try=120, interval=1, allow_missing=True)\n"
    "                    if not done:\n"
    "                        self._set_job_state(job_key, \"failed\", error=f\"任务 {task_id} 未确认完成\", task_ids=task_ids)\n"
    "                        return {\n"
    "                            \"success\": False,\n"
    "                            \"message\": f\"增量转存任务 {task_id} 未确认完成\",\n"
    "                            \"completed_items\": completed,\n"
    "                            \"task_ids\": task_ids,\n"
    "                            \"retryable\": True,\n"
    "                            \"stage\": \"restore_task\",\n"
    "                            \"reason\": \"task_not_done\",\n"
    "                        }\n"
    "                elif not task_id:\n"
    "                    # HTTP/业务成功但无 taskId：不得直接当完成，必须靠远程可见性确认。\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【转存任务】restore_share 未返回 taskId；改走远程确认 stage=remote_verify retryable=true\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                self._set_job_state(job_key, \"task_confirmed\", task_ids=task_ids, group_paths=group_paths)\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【远程确认】开始校验目录 %s 的 %s 个文件\", normalized, len(group))\n"
    "                verified = self._verify_restored_group(api, parent_id, normalized, group, max_try=30, interval=1.0)\n"
    "                if not verified.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                    message = f\"转存任务已完成但目标文件未全部确认：{verified.get('message') or '-'}\"\n"
    "                    self._set_job_state(job_key, \"verifying\", error=message, task_ids=task_ids, group_paths=group_paths)\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"success\": False,\n"
    "                        \"pending_verification\": True,\n"
    "                        \"message\": message,\n"
    "                        \"completed_items\": completed,\n"
    "                        \"task_ids\": task_ids,\n"
    "                        \"retryable\": True,\n"
    "                        \"stage\": \"remote_verify\",\n"
    "                        \"reason\": \"remote_not_found\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                completed.extend(verified.get(\"verified_items\") or group)\n"
    "                self._set_job_state(job_key, \"verified\", task_ids=task_ids, verified_paths=[str(item.get(\"effective_path\") or item.get(\"relative_path\") or item.get(\"name\") or \"\") for item in completed])\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【落盘确认】目录 %s 已确认 %s 个文件可见且大小匹配\", normalized, len(group))\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True, \"message\": f\"增量转存并落盘确认完成，共新增 {len(completed)} 个文件\",\n"
    "                \"completed_items\": completed, \"task_ids\": task_ids,\n"
    "                \"confirmation\": \"所有转存任务完成且目标文件可见性/大小已确认\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"EXCEPTION\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【转存】执行增量转存异常：target=%s\", save_path)\n"
    "            self._set_job_state(job_key, \"failed\", error=str(err), task_ids=task_ids)\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": f\"光鸭增量转存异常: {err}\", \"completed_items\": completed, \"task_ids\": task_ids}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _restore_share(self, share_url: str, save_path: str, probe: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"兼容入口：仍使用文件级恢复，避免整份分享重复转存。\"\"\"\n"
    "        probe = probe or self._inspect_share(share_url)\n"
    "        if not probe.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            return probe\n"
    "        items = self._plan_incremental_files(probe, {})\n"
    "        return self._restore_items(probe, save_path, items)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _root_folder_options(self, raw: bool = False) -> List[Any]:\n"
    "        client, _ = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not client:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            response = client._request(\n"
    "                method=\"POST\",\n"
    "                url=f\"{client.API_BASE_URL}/nd.bizuserres.s/v1/file/get_file_list\",\n"
    "                data={\"parentId\": \"\", \"page\": 0, \"pageSize\": 100, \"orderBy\": 0, \"sortType\": 0, \"fileTypes\": []},\n"
    "                need_auth=True,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            result = []\n"
    "            for value in _extract_result_list(response):\n"
    "                item = _cloud_item(value)\n"
    "                if item and item[\"is_dir\"]:\n"
    "                    row = {\"title\": \"/\" + item[\"name\"], \"value\": \"/\" + item[\"name\"], \"file_id\": item[\"id\"]}\n"
    "                    result.append(row if raw else row[\"value\"])\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _trim_history(history: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        if len(history) <= 500:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        ordered = sorted(history.items(), key=lambda pair: str((pair[1] or {}).get(\"time\") or \"\"), reverse=True)\n"
    "        history.clear()\n"
    "        history.update(dict(ordered[:500]))\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _parse_datetime(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime.datetime]:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return datetime.datetime.strptime(str(value or \"\"), \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _to_int(value: Any, default: int, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> int:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = int(value)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            parsed = default\n"
    "        return max(minimum, min(parsed, maximum))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def stop_service(self) -> None:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            stop = getattr(self, \"_runtime_stop\", None)\n"
    "            if stop is not None:\n"
    "                stop.set()\n"
    "            with type(self)._runtime_generation_lock:\n"
    "                type(self)._runtime_generation += 1\n"
    "            thread = getattr(self, \"_runtime_thread\", None)\n"
    "            if thread and thread.is_alive() and thread is not threading.current_thread():\n"
    "                thread.join(timeout=1.0)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        self._restore_takeover()\n"
    "        self._inspect_cache.clear()\n"
    "\n"
)

    "    def _recognize_media_cached_v208(self, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:\n"
    "        key = self._recognize_cache_key_v208(**kwargs)\n"
    "        if not key:\n"
    "            return MediaChain().recognize_media(**kwargs)\n"
    "\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_recognize_cache_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._recognize_cache_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        cache = getattr(self, \"_recognize_cache_v208\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(cache, dict):\n"
    "            cache = {}\n"
    "            self._recognize_cache_v208 = cache\n"

    "            try:\n"
    "                age = now - float(row.get(\"at\") or 0)\n"

    "                age = 10**9\n"

    "        try:\n"
    "            value = MediaChain().recognize_media(**kwargs)\n"

    "            # timeout / DNS / 5xx 等临时失败不写负缓存。\n"

    "            cache[key] = {\"at\": now, \"value\": value}\n"

    "                ordered = sorted(cache.items(), key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"at\") or 0), reverse=True)[:256]\n"
    "                self._recognize_cache_v208 = dict(ordered)\n"
    "        return self._clone_recognize_value_v208(value)\n"

    "\n"
    "    def _enqueue_external_recall_v208(self, subscribe: Any, reason: str = \"local_candidates_exhausted\") -> bool:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            sid = 0\n"
    "        if sid <= 0:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_external_recall_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._external_recall_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        pending = getattr(self, \"_external_recall_pending_v208\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(pending, dict):\n"
    "            pending = {}\n"
    "            self._external_recall_pending_v208 = pending\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            last = float(pending.get(str(sid)) or 0)\n"
    "            if last and now - last < float(self._external_recall_dedup_seconds_v208):\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【外部补搜】sid=%s reason=%s decision=dedup\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    str(reason or \"\")[:120],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "            pending[str(sid)] = now\n"
    "            if len(pending) > 1000:\n"
    "                self._external_recall_pending_v208 = dict(\n"
    "                    sorted(pending.items(), key=lambda pair: float(pair[1] or 0), reverse=True)[:1000]\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【外部补搜】sid=%s reason=%s decision=enqueue_gying\",\n"
    "            sid,\n"
    "            str(reason or \"\")[:120],\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=\"外部补搜·本地候选耗尽\")\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _share_tombstone_key_v208(self, source_type: str, identity: str) -> str:\n"
    "        return f\"{str(source_type or 'guangya').strip().lower()}|{str(identity or '').strip()}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _is_share_tombstoned_v208(self, source_type: str, identity: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        key = self._share_tombstone_key_v208(source_type, identity)\n"
    "        if not key.endswith(\"|\") and key.split(\"|\", 1)[-1]:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_tombstone_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._tombstone_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        store = getattr(self, \"_share_tombstone_v208\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(store, dict):\n"
    "            store = {}\n"
    "            self._share_tombstone_v208 = store\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            row = dict(store.get(key) or {})\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                until = float(row.get(\"until\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                until = 0\n"
    "            if until > now:\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            if key in store:\n"
    "                store.pop(key, None)\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _mark_share_tombstone_v208(self, source_type: str, identity: str, reason: str, *, temporary: bool = False) -> None:\n"
    "        key = self._share_tombstone_key_v208(source_type, identity)\n"
    "        if not str(identity or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        ttl = self._tombstone_temp_ttl_v208 if temporary else self._tombstone_expired_ttl_v208\n"
    "        until = time.time() + float(ttl)\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_tombstone_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._tombstone_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        store = getattr(self, \"_share_tombstone_v208\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(store, dict):\n"
    "            store = {}\n"
    "            self._share_tombstone_v208 = store\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            store[key] = {\"until\": until, \"reason\": str(reason or \"\")[:240], \"temporary\": bool(temporary)}\n"
    "            if len(store) > 2000:\n"
    "                self._share_tombstone_v208 = dict(\n"
    "                    sorted(store.items(), key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"until\") or 0), reverse=True)[:2000]\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【资源失效】share_id=%s reason=%s ttl=%s\",\n"
    "            str(identity or \"\")[:80],\n"
    "            str(reason or \"\")[:160],\n"
    "            int(ttl),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _begin_failure_batch_v208(self) -> None:\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._failure_batch_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            self._failure_batch_active_v208 = int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0) + 1\n"
    "            if not isinstance(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_v208\", None), list):\n"
    "                self._failure_batch_v208 = []\n"
    "            if not isinstance(getattr(self, \"_diag_batch_v209\", None), list):\n"
    "                self._diag_batch_v209 = []\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            instance = \"\"\n"
    "            getter = getattr(self, \"_instance_id_marker_v209\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(getter):\n"
    "                instance = str(getter() or \"\")\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                instance = str(getattr(self, \"_instance_id_v209\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【批次开始】version=%s build=%s instance=%s\",\n"
    "                str(getattr(self, \"plugin_version\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                str(getattr(self, \"build_id\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                instance or \"-\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _record_transfer_diag_v209(self, subscribe: Any, diag: Dict[str, Any], *, final: bool = True) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Accumulate structured diags for end-of-batch summary (upsert by subscription_run_id).\"\"\"\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._failure_batch_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            if int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0) <= 0:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            rows = getattr(self, \"_diag_batch_v209\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(rows, list):\n"
    "                rows = []\n"
    "                self._diag_batch_v209 = rows\n"
    "            item = dict(diag or {})\n"
    "            item.setdefault(\"sid\", int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0))\n"
    "            item.setdefault(\"subscribe_title\", str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            item[\"_final\"] = bool(final)\n"
    "            run_id = str(item.get(\"subscription_run_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if run_id:\n"
    "                for idx, existing in enumerate(rows):\n"
    "                    if str((existing or {}).get(\"subscription_run_id\") or \"\") == run_id:\n"
    "                        # Prefer final over provisional; otherwise replace in place.\n"
    "                        if (not bool((existing or {}).get(\"_final\"))) or bool(final):\n"
    "                            rows[idx] = item\n"
    "                        return\n"
    "            # Without run_id, still dedupe by sid within the same batch for final rows.\n"
    "            if final and item.get(\"sid\"):\n"
    "                for idx, existing in enumerate(rows):\n"
    "                    if (\n"
    "                        bool((existing or {}).get(\"_final\"))\n"
    "                        and int((existing or {}).get(\"sid\") or 0) == int(item.get(\"sid\") or 0)\n"
    "                        and not str((existing or {}).get(\"subscription_run_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                    ):\n"
    "                        rows[idx] = item\n"
    "                        return\n"
    "            rows.append(item)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _queue_failure_notice_v208(self, subscribe: Any, message: str, *, level: str = \"retryable\") -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"返回 True 表示已进入批次缓冲，调用方不要再即时推送。\n"
    "\n"
    "        注意：这里只缓冲失败文案，不计入最终 batch checked —— 最终计数由\n"
    "        `_trace_transfer_result_v209` → `_record_transfer_diag_v209(final=True)` upsert。\n"
    "        \"\"\"\n"
    "        text = str(message or \"\")\n"
    "        hard = level == \"error\" or any(\n"
    "            token in text for token in (\"认证\", \"登录\", \"Authorization\", \"token 无效\", \"全局不可用\", \"API 不可用\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if hard:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        # Prefer structured diag carried on the current run when available.\n"
    "        diag = None\n"
    "        current = getattr(self, \"_current_subscription_candidate_diags_v209\", None)\n"
    "        if isinstance(current, list) and current:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                from .transfer_diag_v209 import aggregate_subscription_diag\n"
    "                diag = aggregate_subscription_diag(current)\n"
    "                agg_state = str((diag or {}).get(\"final_state\") or (diag or {}).get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "                # Candidate soft-fail while SUCCESS/PENDING/SKIPPED remains: never escalate.\n"
    "                if agg_state in {\"SUCCESS\", \"PENDING\"} or str(agg_state).startswith(\"SKIPPED\"):\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "                if agg_state and not str(agg_state).startswith(\"FAILED\"):\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                diag = None\n"
    "        if not diag:\n"
    "            diag = classify_transfer_message_v209({\"message\": text}, subscribe)\n"
    "        code = str(diag.get(\"reason_code\") or \"\")\n"
    "        # Soft codes: do not create per-item failure rows; final upsert handles counting.\n"
    "        if code in {\n"
    "            \"NO_LOCAL_RESOURCE\", \"NO_SUBSCRIPTION_MATCH\", \"CHANNEL_NO_ACTIONABLE_URL\",\n"
    "            \"RESOURCE_CONTAINS_ONLY_EXISTING_EPISODES\", \"CURRENT_TARGET_ALREADY_SATISFIED\",\n"
    "            \"NO_NEW_ACTION\", \"REMOTE_TASK_PENDING\", \"TRANSFER_ALREADY_RESERVED\",\n"
    "            \"SUPERSEDED_BY_HIGHER_PRIORITY_SOURCE\", \"FUTURE_EPISODE_NOT_DUE\",\n"
    "            \"SUBSCRIPTION_COMPLETED\", \"LIBRARY_ALREADY_SATISFIED\",\n"
    "            \"PARTIAL_TRANSFER_PENDING\", \"COMPLETION_SYNC_PENDING\",\n"
    "            \"TMDB_MATCH\", \"OFFICIAL_ALIAS_MATCH\", \"TITLE_MATCH\",\n"
    "        }:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        # Candidate identity rejects: suppress immediate unless this is already the aggregated FAILED final.\n"
    "        if code in {\"MEDIA_IDENTITY_UNCONFIRMED\", \"TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\", \"SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING\", \"REMOTE_VIDEO_MISSING\"}:\n"
    "            agg_state = str((diag or {}).get(\"final_state\") or (diag or {}).get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if not agg_state.startswith(\"FAILED\"):\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            if isinstance(current, list) and len(current) > 1:\n"
    "                # Multi-candidate run: only final FAILED aggregation may notify once later.\n"
    "                if int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0) > 0:\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._failure_batch_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            if int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0) <= 0:\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "            rows = getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_v208\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(rows, list):\n"
    "                rows = []\n"
    "                self._failure_batch_v208 = rows\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"sid\": int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "                \"name\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"message\": text[:240],\n"
    "                \"diag\": diag,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            # Do NOT call _record_transfer_diag here — avoids double-count with final trace.\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _flush_failure_batch_v208(self) -> None:\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_lock_v208\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._failure_batch_lock_v208 = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            active = int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if active > 0:\n"
    "                self._failure_batch_active_v208 = active - 1\n"
    "            if int(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_active_v208\", 0) or 0) > 0:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            legacy_rows = list(getattr(self, \"_failure_batch_v208\", None) or [])\n"
    "            diag_rows = [\n"
    "                row for row in (getattr(self, \"_diag_batch_v209\", None) or [])\n"
    "                if bool((row or {}).get(\"_final\", True))\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            self._failure_batch_v208 = []\n"
    "            self._diag_batch_v209 = []\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_notify\", False)):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        # Prefer structured diags; fall back to legacy failure rows.\n"
    "        diags = list(diag_rows)\n"
    "        if not diags and legacy_rows:\n"
    "            for row in legacy_rows:\n"
    "                sub = type(\"S\", (), {\"id\": row.get(\"sid\"), \"name\": row.get(\"name\")})()\n"
    "                diags.append(classify_transfer_message_v209({\"message\": row.get(\"message\")}, sub))\n"
    "        if not diags:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        buckets = batch_summary_buckets(diags)\n"
    "        highlights = []\n"
    "        for diag in diags:\n"
    "            code = str(diag.get(\"reason_code\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if code in {\n"
    "                \"TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\", \"SEASON_MISMATCH\", \"SHARE_EXPIRED\",\n"
    "                \"CLOUD_TASK_SUBMIT_FAILED\", \"REMOTE_VERIFY_FAILED\", \"TRANSFER_FAILED\",\n"
    "                \"MEDIA_IDENTITY_UNCONFIRMED\",\n"
    "            }:\n"
    "                evidence = diag.get(\"evidence\") or {}\n"
    "                extra = \"\"\n"
    "                if code == \"SEASON_MISMATCH\":\n"
    "                    extra = f\" 期望 S{evidence.get('expected_season')} 实际 {evidence.get('actual_seasons')}\"\n"
    "                elif code == \"TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\":\n"
    "                    extra = f\" 期望 {evidence.get('expected_tmdb')} 实际 {evidence.get('actual_tmdb')}\"\n"
    "                highlights.append(\n"
    "                    f\"#{diag.get('sid') or evidence.get('subscribe_id') or '-'} \"\n"
    "                    f\"{diag.get('subscribe_title') or evidence.get('subscribe_title') or '-'} \"\n"
    "                    f\"{code}{extra}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            if len(highlights) >= 8:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        # Skip empty-noise-only batches (all NO_LOCAL) unless there are highlights/failures.\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            buckets.get(\"failed\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"identity_reject\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"season_conflict\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"expired\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"success\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"pending\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and buckets.get(\"completed\", 0) <= 0\n"
    "            and not highlights\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            # Still log; do not notify every NO_LOCAL-only round.\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【转存诊断】批次仅本地暂无资源 checked=%s，跳过通知\", buckets.get(\"checked\", 0))\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        text = format_batch_summary(buckets, highlights)\n"
    "        # Hard guard: never emit the old misleading counter wording / titles.\n"
    "        banned_snippets = (\"失败/待补搜\", \"光鸭转存检查完成\", \"本轮处理：\")\n"
    "        if any(token in text for token in banned_snippets):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【通知护栏】结构化汇总含旧格式片段，已拦截发送\")\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if \"失败/待补搜\" in text:\n"
    "            text = text.replace(\"失败/待补搜\", \"真正失败\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                from app.schemas.types import NotificationType\n"
    "                mtype = NotificationType.Plugin\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                mtype = \"Plugin\"\n"
    "            title = \"⚠️ 光鸭转存检查汇总\"\n"
    "            if title == \"⚠️ 光鸭转存检查完成\" or \"检查完成\" in title:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【通知护栏】拦截旧批次标题\")\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            self.post_message(mtype=mtype, title=title, text=text[:1800])\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【通知】批次汇总发送失败：%s\", err)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _run_v1115_mode_batch(self, batch: List[int], trigger: str, mode: str, force: bool = False) -> None:\n"
    "        self._begin_failure_batch_v208()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._run_v1115_mode_batch(batch, trigger, mode, force=force)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._flush_failure_batch_v208()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaProductionSafetyV208Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== channel_sources_v11214.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["channel_sources_v11214"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.14 频道四类来源矩阵补全。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.9.0 的频道 ResourceGroup 已支持光鸭分享、Magnet、ED2K，但没有把同一条 Telegram\n"
    "消息中的迅雷分享纳入索引。此层只扩展“资源发现”，不执行迅雷协议：\n"
    "- 同一消息中的 光鸭分享 / 迅雷分享 / Magnet / ED2K 保持同一个 resource_group_id；\n"
    "- 仅含迅雷链接的消息也保留为频道伪条目；\n"
    "- 迅雷 passcode 只从 URL query 或同一消息上下文提取；\n"
    "- 后续秒传仍完全复用现有 Xunlei JSON / 媒体身份 / 缺集硬栅栏。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import functools\n"
    "import hashlib\n"
    "import html\n"
    "import re\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List\n"
    "from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit, unquote\n"
    "\n"
    "from .channel_sources_v190 import _resource_group_id\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_XUNLEI_CHANNEL_RE = re.compile(r\"https?://pan\\.xunlei\\.com/s/[^\\s\\\"'<>，。；;]+\", re.I)\n"
    "_XUNLEI_CODE_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?:提取码|访问码|密码|口令|pass\\s*code|passcode|pwd)\\s*[:：=]?\\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{1,16})\",\n"
    "    re.I,\n"
    ")\n"
    "_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS_V11214 = 2_000_000\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _decode_channel_blob(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"occurrence discovery 前规范化；与 v190 同语义，本模块自包含供 AST 契约测试。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current = html.unescape(str(value or \"\"))\n"
    "    if len(current) > _CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS_V11214:\n"
    "        current = current[:_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS_V11214]\n"
    "    current = current.replace(\"\\\\/\", \"/\").replace(\"\\\\u002F\", \"/\").replace(\"\\\\u002f\", \"/\")\n"
    "    for _ in range(3):\n"
    "        decoded = unquote(current)\n"
    "        if decoded == current:\n"
    "            break\n"
    "        current = decoded\n"
    "        if len(current) > _CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS_V11214:\n"
    "            current = current[:_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS_V11214]\n"
    "            break\n"
    "    return current\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _clean_xunlei_url_v11214(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return html.unescape(str(value or \"\")).replace(\"\\\\/\", \"/\").strip().rstrip(\".,，。;；)）]】}\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _xunlei_channel_rows_v11214(context_html: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"从一个消息上下文提取稳定 Xunlei share_id/passcode，不跨消息借验证码。\"\"\"\n"
    "    decoded = _decode_channel_blob(context_html)\n"
    "    rows: List[Dict[str, str]] = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for matched in _XUNLEI_CHANNEL_RE.finditer(decoded):\n"
    "        uri = _clean_xunlei_url_v11214(matched.group(0))\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = urlsplit(uri)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        share = re.search(r\"^/s/([^/?#]+)\", parsed.path or \"\", re.I)\n"
    "        if not share:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        share_id = str(share.group(1) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not share_id:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        query = parse_qs(parsed.query or \"\")\n"
    "        passcode = \"\"\n"
    "        for key in (\"pwd\", \"passcode\", \"pass_code\", \"code\"):\n"
    "            values = query.get(key) or []\n"
    "            if values and str(values[0] or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                passcode = str(values[0]).strip()\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        if not passcode:\n"
    "            code = _XUNLEI_CODE_RE.search(decoded)\n"
    "            if code:\n"
    "                passcode = str(code.group(1) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        key = (share_id, passcode)\n"
    "        if key in seen:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        seen.add(key)\n"
    "        rows.append({\n"
    "            \"type\": \"xunlei\",\n"
    "            \"uri\": uri,\n"
    "            \"identity\": share_id,\n"
    "            \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "            \"passcode\": passcode,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "    return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _candidate_types_v11214(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:\n"
    "    ordered: List[str] = []\n"
    "    if entry.get(\"xunlei_sources\"):\n"
    "        ordered.append(\"xunlei\")\n"
    "    if str(entry.get(\"share_url\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "        ordered.append(\"guangya\")\n"
    "    for item in entry.get(\"external_sources\") or []:\n"
    "        value = str((item or {}).get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        if value in {\"magnet\", \"ed2k\"} and value not in ordered:\n"
    "            ordered.append(value)\n"
    "    return ordered\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def install_channel_source_matrix_v11214(legacy_module: Any):\n"
    "    \"\"\"在 v1.9.0 ResourceGroup 解析器外再补 Xunlei；热重载重复安装是 no-op。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current = getattr(legacy_module, \"_extract_channel_entries\", None)\n"
    "    if not callable(current):\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "    if getattr(current, \"_guangya_channel_source_matrix_v11214\", False):\n"
    "        return current\n"
    "    original = current\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(original)\n"
    "    def patched_extract(page_text: str, source_url: str, source_label: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        entries = [dict(row or {}) for row in (original(page_text, source_url, source_label) or [])]\n"
    "        # URL-encoded 迅雷链接必须在 finditer 之前 decode，否则永远匹配不到\n"
    "        decoded = _decode_channel_blob(page_text)\n"
    "        occurrences = list(_XUNLEI_CHANNEL_RE.finditer(decoded))\n"
    "        if not occurrences:\n"
    "            for row in entries:\n"
    "                row[\"candidate_types\"] = _candidate_types_v11214(row)\n"
    "            return entries\n"
    "\n"
    "        groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for match in occurrences:\n"
    "            context_html = legacy_module._message_context_html(decoded, match.start())\n"
    "            # 上下文同样基于已 decode 文本，pwd query 才能被 urlsplit/parse_qs 识别\n"
    "            context = legacy_module._html_to_text(context_html)\n"
    "            xunlei = _xunlei_channel_rows_v11214(context_html)\n"
    "            if not xunlei:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            metadata = dict(legacy_module._entry_metadata(context, context_html) or {})\n"
    "            message_id = str(metadata.get(\"message_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            group_id = _resource_group_id(source_url, message_id, context)\n"
    "            group = groups.setdefault(group_id, {\n"
    "                \"resource_group_id\": group_id,\n"
    "                \"text\": context[:4000],\n"
    "                \"source_url\": source_url,\n"
    "                \"source_label\": source_label,\n"
    "                \"priority\": 0 if \"regeng\" in str(source_url or \"\").lower() else 1,\n"
    "                \"stale\": False,\n"
    "                \"cached_index\": False,\n"
    "                \"external_sources\": [],\n"
    "                \"xunlei_sources\": [],\n"
    "                **metadata,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            seen = {\n"
    "                (str(item.get(\"share_id\") or \"\"), str(item.get(\"passcode\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                for item in group.get(\"xunlei_sources\") or []\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            for item in xunlei:\n"
    "                key = (str(item.get(\"share_id\") or \"\"), str(item.get(\"passcode\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                if key not in seen:\n"
    "                    seen.add(key)\n"
    "                    group[\"xunlei_sources\"].append(dict(item))\n"
    "\n"
    "        attached = set()\n"
    "        for entry in entries:\n"
    "            entry_message = str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            entry_text = str(entry.get(\"text\") or \"\")\n"
    "            for group_id, group in groups.items():\n"
    "                same = bool(entry_message and entry_message == str(group.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                if not same and not entry_message and entry_text == str(group.get(\"text\") or \"\"):\n"
    "                    same = True\n"
    "                if not same and str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\") == group_id:\n"
    "                    same = True\n"
    "                if not same:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                existing = list(entry.get(\"xunlei_sources\") or [])\n"
    "                seen = {(str(v.get(\"share_id\") or \"\"), str(v.get(\"passcode\") or \"\")) for v in existing if isinstance(v, dict)}\n"
    "                for item in group.get(\"xunlei_sources\") or []:\n"
    "                    key = (str(item.get(\"share_id\") or \"\"), str(item.get(\"passcode\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                    if key not in seen:\n"
    "                        seen.add(key)\n"
    "                        existing.append(dict(item))\n"
    "                entry[\"xunlei_sources\"] = existing\n"
    "                entry[\"resource_group_id\"] = str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or group_id)\n"
    "                attached.add(group_id)\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            entry[\"candidate_types\"] = _candidate_types_v11214(entry)\n"
    "\n"
    "        for group_id, group in groups.items():\n"
    "            if group_id in attached:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            pseudo = {\n"
    "                **group,\n"
    "                \"share_url\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"share_id\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"link_style\": \"迅雷资源\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            pseudo[\"candidate_types\"] = _candidate_types_v11214(pseudo)\n"
    "            entries.append(pseudo)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 同一 ResourceGroup 最多保留一条伪记录，避免重复消息窗口造成二次索引。\n"
    "        result: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        seen_keys = set()\n"
    "        for entry in entries:\n"
    "            group_id = str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            share_url = str(entry.get(\"share_url\") or \"\")\n"
    "            marker = (group_id, share_url, str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            if marker in seen_keys and not share_url:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            seen_keys.add(marker)\n"
    "            result.append(entry)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    patched_extract._guangya_channel_source_matrix_v11214 = True\n"
    "    patched_extract._guangya_original_extract_channel_entries = original\n"
    "    legacy_module._extract_channel_entries = patched_extract\n"
    "    return patched_extract\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"install_channel_source_matrix_v11214\",\n"
    "    \"_xunlei_channel_rows_v11214\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== page_auth_v172.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["page_auth_v172"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手页面 API 与页面故障隔离兼容层。\n"
    "\n"
    "MoviePilot V3 插件页面使用当前登录会话的 Bearer 鉴权。页面事件只传业务参数，\n"
    "不把 API_TOKEN 作为 token/apikey 注入按钮参数。\n"
    "\n"
    "状态页的“立即检查缺集/复查待落盘”不能在 HTTP 请求线程里同步跑完整频道读取、\n"
    "分享解析和转存流程，否则前端会在网络稍慢时提示“服务无响应”。本层把这两类操作\n"
    "送入插件已经存在的可靠后台合并队列，并立即返回标准三段式 Response envelope；\n"
    "其余状态页操作也统一收口为严格的 success/message/data 三字段响应。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.10.2 追加页面故障隔离：新版控制台会同时读取自检、资源计划、频道缓存、Provider\n"
    "配置和迅雷运行态。升级后任意一份旧缓存形态异常，或者任意运行时探针抛错，都不应让\n"
    "MoviePilot 整个插件页返回“数据加载失败”。本层在不改变转存业务逻辑的前提下，对这些\n"
    "只读页面数据做 fail-soft 包装：失败模块降级显示，控制台本身继续可用，并把详细异常写入\n"
    "插件日志供排障。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import functools\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_STATUS_ACTION_PATHS = {\n"
    "    \"/check_missing\",\n"
    "    \"/recheck_pending\",\n"
    "    \"/cancel_pending\",\n"
    "    \"/reset_state\",\n"
    "    \"/release_native\",\n"
    "    \"/daily_summary\",\n"
    "    \"/clear_plugin_logs\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "_ASYNC_STATUS_PATHS = {\n"
    "    \"/check_missing\": \"状态页立即检查缺集\",\n"
    "    \"/recheck_pending\": \"状态页复查待落盘\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _response_envelope(result: Any, default_message: str = \"操作完成\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"把旧版插件 API 返回值规范成 MoviePilot V3 严格三段式响应。\"\"\"\n"
    "    if isinstance(result, dict):\n"
    "        success = bool(result.get(\"success\", True))\n"
    "        message = str(result.get(\"message\") or (default_message if success else \"操作失败\"))\n"
    "        data = {\n"
    "            key: value\n"
    "            for key, value in result.items()\n"
    "            if key not in {\"success\", \"message\"}\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": success,\n"
    "            \"message\": message,\n"
    "            \"data\": data or None,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"success\": True,\n"
    "        \"message\": default_message,\n"
    "        \"data\": result,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _subscription_id(args: tuple, kwargs: dict) -> int:\n"
    "    \"\"\"从 FastAPI 绑定参数中稳健取得 subscribe_id。\"\"\"\n"
    "    value = kwargs.get(\"subscribe_id\")\n"
    "    if value in (None, \"\") and args:\n"
    "        value = args[0]\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return int(value or 0)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return 0\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _queue_status_action(path: str, endpoint: Any, *args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"校验状态页耗时操作并提交现有可靠后台队列，HTTP 立即返回。\"\"\"\n"
    "    plugin = getattr(endpoint, \"__self__\", None)\n"
    "    if plugin is None:\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"插件运行实例不可用，请刷新页面后重试\",\n"
    "            \"data\": None,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    sid = _subscription_id(args, kwargs)\n"
    "    subscribe = plugin._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "    if not sid or subscribe is None:\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"订阅不存在\",\n"
    "            \"data\": {\"subscribe_id\": sid} if sid else None,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    selected = {\n"
    "        int(value)\n"
    "        for value in (getattr(plugin, \"_selected_subscriptions\", None) or [])\n"
    "        if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "    }\n"
    "    if sid not in selected:\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"该订阅当前不是光鸭固定转存路线\",\n"
    "            \"data\": {\"subscribe_id\": sid},\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    runtime_check = getattr(plugin, \"_runtime_is_current\", None)\n"
    "    if callable(runtime_check) and not runtime_check():\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"插件已热更新，请刷新当前页面后重试\",\n"
    "            \"data\": {\"subscribe_id\": sid},\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # “立即检查缺集”保留原人工门禁：已有待落盘任务时不能强制重放旧消息。\n"
    "    if path == \"/check_missing\":\n"
    "        manual_guard = getattr(plugin, \"_manual_transfer_guard\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(manual_guard):\n"
    "            guard = manual_guard(subscribe)\n"
    "            if guard:\n"
    "                return _response_envelope(guard, default_message=\"检查被安全门禁阻止\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    trigger = _ASYNC_STATUS_PATHS[path]\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        plugin._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=trigger)\n"
    "        record_health = getattr(plugin, \"_record_route_health\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(record_health):\n"
    "            record_health(\n"
    "                last_page_action_queued=trigger,\n"
    "                last_page_action_id=sid,\n"
    "                last_page_action_at=plugin._now_text(),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        plugin._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【页面操作】%s #%s %s 已进入后台队列，HTTP 请求立即返回\",\n"
    "            trigger,\n"
    "            sid,\n"
    "            getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "    except Exception as err:\n"
    "        plugin._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"EXCEPTION\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【页面操作】%s #%s 入队失败：%s\",\n"
    "            trigger,\n"
    "            sid,\n"
    "            err,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": f\"后台任务提交失败：{err}\",\n"
    "            \"data\": {\"subscribe_id\": sid},\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"success\": True,\n"
    "        \"message\": \"已进入后台检查队列，请稍后查看状态卡片或插件日志\",\n"
    "        \"data\": {\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "            \"queued\": True,\n"
    "            \"action\": path.lstrip(\"/\"),\n"
    "        },\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _wrap_status_endpoint(path: str, endpoint: Any) -> Any:\n"
    "    \"\"\"保持原 FastAPI 签名，同时统一状态页操作响应。\"\"\"\n"
    "    if path in _ASYNC_STATUS_PATHS:\n"
    "        @functools.wraps(endpoint)\n"
    "        def queued_endpoint(*args, **kwargs):\n"
    "            return _queue_status_action(path, endpoint, *args, **kwargs)\n"
    "\n"
    "        return queued_endpoint\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(endpoint)\n"
    "    def response_endpoint(*args, **kwargs):\n"
    "        return _response_envelope(endpoint(*args, **kwargs))\n"
    "\n"
    "    return response_endpoint\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def force_bear_auth(routes: Any) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"统一 Bearer 鉴权，并给状态页按钮安装非阻塞/标准响应适配器。\"\"\"\n"
    "    result: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "    for route in list(routes or []):\n"
    "        if not isinstance(route, dict):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        item = dict(route)\n"
    "        item[\"auth\"] = \"bear\"\n"
    "        path = str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\")\n"
    "        endpoint = item.get(\"endpoint\")\n"
    "        if path in _STATUS_ACTION_PATHS and callable(endpoint):\n"
    "            item[\"endpoint\"] = _wrap_status_endpoint(path, endpoint)\n"
    "        result.append(item)\n"
    "    return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def strip_page_api_secrets(node: Any) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"递归移除状态页按钮中的 token/apikey，仅保留业务参数。\"\"\"\n"
    "    if isinstance(node, dict):\n"
    "        events = node.get(\"events\")\n"
    "        if isinstance(events, dict):\n"
    "            click = events.get(\"click\")\n"
    "            if isinstance(click, dict) and str(click.get(\"api\") or \"\").startswith(\n"
    "                \"plugin/GuangYaTransferAssistant/\"\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                params = click.get(\"params\")\n"
    "                if isinstance(params, dict):\n"
    "                    params.pop(\"token\", None)\n"
    "                    params.pop(\"apikey\", None)\n"
    "        for value in node.values():\n"
    "            strip_page_api_secrets(value)\n"
    "    elif isinstance(node, list):\n"
    "        for value in node:\n"
    "            strip_page_api_secrets(value)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "# v1.10.2 页面数据故障隔离\n"
    "# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "\n"
    "def _page_log_error(plugin: Any, stage: str, err: Exception) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"页面降级只记录异常，不让记录异常本身再次影响页面返回。\"\"\"\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        log = getattr(plugin, \"_plugin_log\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(log):\n"
    "            log(\n"
    "                \"EXCEPTION\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【页面降级】%s 读取失败，已隔离该模块：%s\",\n"
    "                stage,\n"
    "                err,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        pass\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _page_mark_error(plugin: Any, stage: str, err: Exception) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"记录当前一次页面构建的降级模块名称，前端只展示模块名而不泄露异常原文。\"\"\"\n"
    "    _page_log_error(plugin, stage, err)\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        rows = getattr(plugin, \"_page_resilience_errors_v1102\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(rows, list):\n"
    "            rows = []\n"
    "            setattr(plugin, \"_page_resilience_errors_v1102\", rows)\n"
    "        if stage not in rows:\n"
    "            rows.append(stage)\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        pass\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _fallback_overview(plugin: Any, stage: str = \"状态汇总\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"提供新控制台所需的完整最小字段，避免二次 KeyError。\"\"\"\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        selected = len(list(getattr(plugin, \"_selected_subscriptions\", None) or []))\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        selected = 0\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"overall\": \"warning\",\n"
    "        \"healthy\": False,\n"
    "        \"attention_count\": 0,\n"
    "        \"selected\": selected,\n"
    "        \"channel_count\": 0,\n"
    "        \"channel_updated\": \"-\",\n"
    "        \"channel_errors\": 0,\n"
    "        \"sources\": {\n"
    "            \"total\": 0,\n"
    "            \"magnet\": 0,\n"
    "            \"ed2k\": 0,\n"
    "            \"pending\": 0,\n"
    "            \"running\": 0,\n"
    "            \"completed\": 0,\n"
    "            \"failed\": 0,\n"
    "            \"review\": 0,\n"
    "        },\n"
    "        \"critical_checks\": [],\n"
    "        \"warning_checks\": [{\n"
    "            \"key\": \"page_data_degraded\",\n"
    "            \"label\": \"页面状态数据已降级\",\n"
    "            \"detail\": f\"{stage}暂时不可用，后台转存任务不会因此停止\",\n"
    "            \"critical\": False,\n"
    "            \"ok\": False,\n"
    "        }],\n"
    "        \"attention_sources\": [],\n"
    "        \"failed_transfer_rows\": [],\n"
    "        \"active_sources\": [],\n"
    "        \"active_transfer_rows\": [],\n"
    "        \"waiting_resource_count\": 0,\n"
    "        \"resource_plan_count\": 0,\n"
    "        \"version\": str(getattr(plugin, \"plugin_version\", \"\")),\n"
    "        \"build\": str(getattr(plugin, \"build_id\", \"\")),\n"
    "        \"viewing\": {\n"
    "            \"enabled\": bool(getattr(plugin, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)),\n"
    "            \"active_node\": \"\",\n"
    "            \"status\": \"unknown\",\n"
    "            \"verified\": False,\n"
    "            \"login_mode\": \"\",\n"
    "        },\n"
    "        \"xunlei_flash\": {\n"
    "            \"enabled\": bool(getattr(plugin, \"_xunlei_flash_enabled\", True)),\n"
    "            \"completed\": 0,\n"
    "            \"failed\": 0,\n"
    "        },\n"
    "        \"page_degraded\": True,\n"
    "        \"page_degraded_stage\": stage,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _page_fallback_cards(plugin: Any, stage: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"即便主控制台构建异常，也返回 MoviePilot 能渲染的最小页面而不是 HTTP 失败。\"\"\"\n"
    "    version = str(getattr(plugin, \"plugin_version\", \"\") or \"-\")\n"
    "    build = str(getattr(plugin, \"build_id\", \"\") or \"-\")\n"
    "    return [\n"
    "        {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\n"
    "                \"type\": \"warning\",\n"
    "                \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                \"title\": \"控制台已降级显示\",\n"
    "                \"text\": (\n"
    "                    f\"{stage}读取异常，已隔离故障模块。后台转存逻辑不会因页面异常被停止。\"\n"
    "                    \"请先打开插件设置检查配置，或查看后台日志中的【页面降级】记录。\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "                \"class\": \"mb-3\",\n"
    "                \"style\": \"border-radius:16px;\",\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        },\n"
    "        {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"

    "                {\"component\": \"VCardTitle\", \"text\": \"光鸭转存助手\"},\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": f\"v{version} · build {build} · 页面保护模式已生效\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        },\n"
    "    ]\n"

    "\n"
    "def _install_page_resilience_v1102() -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"给已加载的 UI mixin 安装 fail-soft 包装；重复导入时保持幂等。\"\"\"\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        from .status_ui_v191 import GuangYaStatusUiMixin\n"
    "        from .console_ui_v1100 import GuangYaConsoleUiV1100Mixin\n"
    "        from .channel_ui_v1101 import GuangYaChannelUiV1101Mixin\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        # 极早期兼容环境缺少某个新版 UI 模块时，不影响插件原有导入路径。\n"
    "        return\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 1) 状态汇总是新版控制台的总入口。旧缓存/ResourcePlan/自检任一异常都只返回降级数据。\n"
    "    original_overview = getattr(GuangYaStatusUiMixin, \"_status_overview_v191\", None)\n"
    "    if callable(original_overview) and not getattr(original_overview, \"_guangya_page_resilience_v1102\", False):\n"
    "        @functools.wraps(original_overview)\n"
    "        def safe_overview(self, *args, **kwargs):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                result = original_overview(self, *args, **kwargs)\n"
    "                if not isinstance(result, dict):\n"
    "                    raise TypeError(\"status overview is not a dict\")\n"
    "                return result\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                _page_mark_error(self, \"状态汇总\", err)\n"
    "                return _fallback_overview(self, \"状态汇总\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        safe_overview._guangya_page_resilience_v1102 = True\n"
    "        GuangYaStatusUiMixin._status_overview_v191 = safe_overview\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 2) Provider 定义解析此前没有 try/except，单行历史配置格式错误即可拖死整页。\n"
    "    original_health = getattr(GuangYaConsoleUiV1100Mixin, \"_runtime_health_rows\", None)\n"
    "    if callable(original_health) and not getattr(original_health, \"_guangya_page_resilience_v1102\", False):\n"
    "        @functools.wraps(original_health)\n"
    "        def safe_health(self, *args, **kwargs):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                result = original_health(self, *args, **kwargs)\n"
    "                return list(result or [])\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                _page_mark_error(self, \"资源来源运行态\", err)\n"
    "                return [{\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 12},\n"
    "                    \"content\": [{\n"
    "                        \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                            \"type\": \"warning\",\n"
    "                            \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                            \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                            \"text\": \"资源来源运行态暂时无法读取，已跳过该模块；可继续使用其它控制台功能。\",\n"
    "                            \"style\": \"border-radius:14px;\",\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                    }],\n"
    "                }]\n"
    "\n"
    "        safe_health._guangya_page_resilience_v1102 = True\n"
    "        GuangYaConsoleUiV1100Mixin._runtime_health_rows = safe_health\n"
    "\n"

    "    def _external_resource_allowed(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        entry: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Magnet/ED2K 在 resolve 前只做候选，不用帖子噪声提前淘汰真实可用资源。\"\"\"\n"
    "        return True, \"外部候选待光鸭解析后校验订阅规则\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _resolved_probe(resolve_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") or {}\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        if isinstance(bt_info, dict):\n"
    "            top_name = str(bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if top_name:\n"
    "                rows.append({\"relative_path\": top_name, \"name\": top_name})\n"
    "            subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") or []\n"
    "            if isinstance(subfiles, list):\n"
    "                for raw in subfiles[:1000]:\n"
    "                    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "                    name = str(raw.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                    if name:\n"
    "                        rows.append({\"relative_path\": name, \"name\": name})\n"
    "        return {\"files\": rows}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        resolved = super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe)\n"
    "        resolve_data = dict(resolved.get(\"resolve_data\") or {})\n"
    "        descriptor = \"\\n\".join(\n"
    "            value for value in (\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"label\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                str(resolved.get(\"resolved_name\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            ) if value\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        entry = {\"text\": descriptor}\n"
    "        allowed, reason = self._subscription_resource_allowed(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            entry,\n"
    "            self._resolved_probe(resolve_data),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not allowed:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"{_RULE_MISMATCH_PREFIX}{reason}\")\n"
    "        return resolved\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _mark_offline_failure(\n"
    "        self,\n"

    "                str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                state=\"failed\",\n"
    "                last_error=f\"订阅规则不匹配：{detail}\"[:500],\n"

    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【候选过滤】来源 %s resolve 后不满足订阅规则，转向下一候选：%s\",\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                detail,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return updated\n"
    "        return super()._mark_offline_failure(\n"
    "            source,\n"
    "            error,\n"
    "            attempt_increment=attempt_increment,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["auto_recovery_v11224"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.24 自动恢复与识别文件名前缀层。\n"
    "\n"
    "修复三个实机回归：\n"
    "1. 频道帖子/分享链接可能持续追加新集，processed_entries 不能永久阻止重新读取；\n"

    "_FORBIDDEN_NAME_V11224 = re.compile(r\"[\\\\/:*?\\\"<>|\\x00-\\x1f]+\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _norm_name_v11224(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return re.sub(r\"[^0-9a-z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+\", \"\", str(value or \"\").casefold())\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _safe_name_v11224(value: Any, limit: int = 120) -> str:\n"
    "    text = _FORBIDDEN_NAME_V11224.sub(\" \", str(value or \"\"))\n"
    "    text = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", text).strip(\" .-_\")\n"
    "    return text[: max(1, int(limit or 120))].strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _prefix_name_v11224(original: Any, prefix: Any, *, limit: int = 240) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"保留原文件名/扩展名，把识别目录名稳定放到最前面并避免重复前缀。\"\"\"\n"
    "    base = _FORBIDDEN_NAME_V11224.sub(\" \", str(original or \"\")).strip()\n"
    "    marker = _safe_name_v11224(prefix)\n"
    "    if not base:\n"
    "        return marker[:limit]\n"
    "    if not marker:\n"
    "        return base[:limit]\n"
    "    base_norm = _norm_name_v11224(base)\n"
    "    marker_norm = _norm_name_v11224(marker)\n"
    "    if marker_norm and base_norm.startswith(marker_norm):\n"
    "        return base[:limit]\n"
    "    joiner = f\"{marker} - \"\n"
    "    available = max(1, int(limit or 240) - len(joiner))\n"
    "    return f\"{joiner}{base[:available].rstrip(' .')}\"[:limit]\n"
    "\n"

    "    plugin_version = \"1.12.24\"\n"

    "    _off_day_recovery_minutes_v11224 = 60\n"

    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if bool(self._movie_transfer_confirmed(subscribe)):\n"
    "                    return set()\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"

    "                pass\n"

    "                state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "                # new/retry 只是候选，completed 只有真实电影完成事实才能阻断；\n"
    "                # dispatching/submitted/queued/waiting 才表示当前确有一个执行中的来源。\n"

    "                    return set()\n"
    "            return {\"movie\"}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return set(self._uncovered_missing_v1125(subscribe) or set())\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return set()\n"

    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 频道增长分享：有缺口时允许周期复核，真正文件级去重仍在后面。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _entry_processed(self, entry: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any = None) -> bool:\n"
    "        processed = bool(super()._entry_processed(entry, subscribe))\n"
    "        if not processed or subscribe is None:\n"
    "            return processed\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            gap = self._recovery_uncovered_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            gap = set()\n"

    "            return True\n"
    "        key = self._processed_entry_key(entry, subscribe)\n"

    "        status = str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"

    "        if status not in {\"synced\", \"no_new_episode\", \"transferred\", \"legacy_synced\", \"processed\"}:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        updated = None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            updated = self._parse_datetime(row.get(\"time\"))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            updated = None\n"
    "        if updated is not None:\n"
    "            age = (datetime.datetime.now() - updated).total_seconds()\n"

    "                return True\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道增长复核v1.12.24】#%s %s 仍有缺口=%s；已处理分享达到复核窗口，重新读取分享内容，文件级库存继续防重复\",\n"
    "            int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "            str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \",\".join(str(v) for v in sorted(gap, key=str)) or \"movie\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # handled 静默阻断恢复：真实缺口存在时继续观影后备来源。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        force: bool = False,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            force=force,\n"
    "            refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "        ) or {})\n"
    "        if \"viewing_external\" in result or bool(result.get(\"completed\")) or bool(result.get(\"pending\")):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            allowed, _ = self._subscription_static_guard(subscribe)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            allowed = True\n"
    "        if not allowed:\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        gap = self._recovery_uncovered_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not gap:\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        reader = getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_value_v1115\", None)\n"
    "        mode = str(reader() if callable(reader) else getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_v1115\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if bool(result.get(\"handled\")):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【自动恢复v1.12.24】#%s %s 前序返回 handled=True 但真实缺口仍存在=%s；不再把 handled 当作完成事实\",\n"
    "                int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "                str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \",\".join(str(v) for v in sorted(gap, key=str)) or \"movie\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 5 分钟 channel_event 仍保持纯被动；把 handled 修正为 False，让下一次缓存补偿能继续恢复。\n"
    "        if mode == \"channel_event\":\n"
    "            if bool(result.get(\"handled\")):\n"
    "                result[\"handled\"] = False\n"
    "                result[\"message\"] = f\"{str(result.get('message') or '频道本轮未形成真实覆盖')}；仍有真实缺口，保留自动恢复\"\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_provider_auto_search\", True)):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            viewing = dict(self._dispatch_viewing_external_v1113(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【自动恢复v1.12.24】#%s %s 继续观影后备来源异常：%s\",\n"
    "                int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "                str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                str(err)[:300],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            viewing = {\"success\": False, \"actions\": [], \"message\": str(err)}\n"
    "        if viewing.get(\"actions\"):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                **result,\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"viewing_external\": viewing,\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"{str(result.get('message') or '前序链未形成真实覆盖')}；{str(viewing.get('message') or '观影后备来源已入队')}\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        # 没有真实入队就不再伪装成“已处理完成”，下一轮仍可重试。\n"
    "        result[\"handled\"] = False\n"
    "        result[\"viewing_external\"] = viewing\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 非更新日/提前放出：每 60 分钟只补 off-day / unscheduled / 最晚明日 next episode。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _v11224_iso_date(value: Any):\n"
    "        text = str(value or \"\").strip()[:10]\n"
    "        if not text:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return datetime.date.fromisoformat(text)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _smart_pull_due_ids_v1125(self) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        due = set(int(v) for v in (super()._smart_pull_due_ids_v1125() or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            search_state = dict(self._external_search_state_v1114() or {})\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            search_state = {}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            gate_state = dict(self.get_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\") or {})\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            gate_state = {}\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        today = datetime.date.today()\n"
    "        added: List[int] = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125() or []:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid <= 0 or sid in due or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            uncovered = set(self._recovery_uncovered_v11224(subscribe) or set())\n"
    "            if not uncovered:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row = dict(gate_state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "            recovery = {\n"
    "                int(v) for v in [*(row.get(\"off_day_missing\") or []), *(row.get(\"unscheduled_missing\") or [])]\n"
    "                if str(v).isdigit() and int(v) > 0\n"
    "            }.intersection({int(v) for v in uncovered if isinstance(v, int) or str(v).isdigit()})\n"
    "            next_episode = int(row.get(\"next_episode\") or 0)\n"
    "            next_date = self._v11224_iso_date(row.get(\"next_air_at\"))\n"
    "            if next_episode > 0 and next_episode in uncovered and next_date and next_date <= today + datetime.timedelta(days=1):\n"
    "                recovery.add(next_episode)\n"
    "            if not recovery:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            state_row = dict(search_state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                last_at = float(state_row.get(\"last_at\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                last_at = 0.0\n"
    "            cooldown = max(1800, int(self._off_day_recovery_minutes_v11224) * 60)\n"
    "            if last_at and now - last_at < cooldown:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            due.add(sid)\n"
    "            added.append(sid)\n"
    "        if added:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【提前资源补漏v1.12.24】非严格更新日仍有可恢复缺口，加入每小时主动检查：%s\",\n"
    "                \",\".join(f\"#{sid}\" for sid in sorted(added)),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return sorted(due)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 命名：实际识别文件夹名必须出现在最前面。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _recognition_folder_name_v11224(self, subscribe: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        target = str(self._target_path(subscribe) or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if bool(getattr(self, \"_create_media_folder\", False)) and target:\n"
    "            folder = target.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "            if folder:\n"
    "                return _safe_name_v11224(folder)\n"
    "        name = _safe_name_v11224(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        year = _safe_name_v11224(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        return _safe_name_v11224(f\"{name} ({year})\" if name and year else name)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_prefix_v11224(self, subscribe: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        prefix = self._recognition_folder_name_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        season = getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None)\n"
    "        if season not in (None, \"\"):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                prefix = _safe_name_v11224(f\"{prefix} S{int(season):02d}\")\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        return prefix\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        resolved = dict(super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        original = str(\n"
    "            source.get(\"original_resolved_name\")\n"
    "            or resolved.get(\"resolved_name\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"resolved_name\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"name\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"label\")\n"
    "            or \"\"\n"
    "        ).strip()\n"
    "        prefix = self._recognition_folder_name_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        desired = _prefix_name_v11224(original, prefix)\n"
    "        if desired and desired != original:\n"
    "            source[\"label\"] = desired\n"
    "            source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if source_id:\n"
    "                self._update_source(\n"
    "                    source_id,\n"
    "                    requested_name=desired,\n"
    "                    original_resolved_name=original[:300],\n"
    "                    recognition_prefix_v11224=prefix[:140],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【识别命名v1.12.24】云添加名称：识别目录=%s 原名=%s 新名=%s\",\n"
    "                prefix[:140] or \"-\",\n"
    "                original[:180] or \"-\",\n"
    "                desired[:220],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return resolved\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _rapid_transfer_xunlei_file(self, subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        prepared = dict(row or {})\n"
    "        old_name = str(prepared.get(\"name\") or str(prepared.get(\"path\") or \"\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1] or \"file\").strip()\n"
    "        prefix = self._xunlei_prefix_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        desired = _prefix_name_v11224(old_name, prefix)\n"
    "        if desired and desired != old_name:\n"
    "            prepared[\"name\"] = desired\n"
    "            raw_path = str(prepared.get(\"path\") or old_name).replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n"
    "            parent = raw_path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0] if \"/\" in raw_path else \"\"\n"
    "            prepared[\"path\"] = f\"{parent}/{desired}\" if parent else desired\n"
    "        return dict(super()._rapid_transfer_xunlei_file(subscribe, prepared) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _restore_subscribe_v11224(self, job_key: str = \"\") -> Any:\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_core_pipeline_local_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        subscribe = getattr(local, \"subscribe\", None) if local is not None else None\n"
    "        if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "            return subscribe\n"
    "        if job_key:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                row = dict(self._get_job_state(job_key) or {})\n"
    "                sid = int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "                if sid > 0:\n"
    "                    return self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _rename_ok_v11224(value: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        if value is True:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        if not isinstance(value, dict) or value.get(\"error\"):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return value.get(\"code\") in (None, 0, \"0\", 200, \"200\") and str(value.get(\"msg\") or value.get(\"message\") or \"\").lower() not in {\"error\", \"failed\", \"fail\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _rename_restored_media_v11224(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        save_path: str,\n"
    "        items: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]],\n"
    "    ) -> int:\n"
    "        if subscribe is None:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        prefix = self._recognition_folder_name_v11224(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not prefix:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        client, api = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not client or not api or not callable(getattr(client, \"rename\", None)):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        groups: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n"
    "        for item in items or []:\n"
    "            path = str(item.get(\"effective_path\") or item.get(\"relative_path\") or item.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if not (_is_video(path) or _is_subtitle(path)):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            groups.setdefault(str(item.get(\"target_parent\") or \"\"), []).append(dict(item))\n"
    "        renamed = 0\n"
    "        base = str(save_path or \"/\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n"
    "        for relative_parent, group in groups.items():\n"
    "            parent = _safe_relative_path(relative_parent)\n"
    "            folder_path = (base.rstrip(\"/\") + (\"/\" + parent if parent else \"\")) or \"/\"\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                folder = api.get_item(Path(folder_path)) if callable(getattr(api, \"get_item\", None)) else None\n"
    "                if folder is None:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                remote_rows = list(api.list(folder) or []) if callable(getattr(api, \"list\", None)) else []\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【识别命名v1.12.24】读取已转存目录失败：%s (%s)\", folder_path, str(err)[:180])\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            remote_by_name = {str(getattr(row, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"): row for row in remote_rows}\n"
    "            for item in group:\n"
    "                path = str(item.get(\"effective_path\") or item.get(\"relative_path\") or item.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n"
    "                old_name = path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "                desired = _prefix_name_v11224(old_name, prefix)\n"
    "                if not desired or desired == old_name:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                remote = remote_by_name.get(old_name)\n"
    "                file_id = str(getattr(remote, \"fileid\", \"\") or \"\") if remote is not None else \"\"\n"
    "                if not file_id:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    response = client.rename(file_id, desired)\n"
    "                    if self._rename_ok_v11224(response):\n"
    "                        renamed += 1\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【识别命名v1.12.24】分享落盘后重命名：%s -> %s\",\n"
    "                            old_name[:180],\n"
    "                            desired[:220],\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【识别命名v1.12.24】分享已成功但重命名未确认：%s -> %s\", old_name[:160], desired[:200])\n"
    "                except Exception as err:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【识别命名v1.12.24】分享已成功但重命名异常：%s\", str(err)[:220])\n"
    "        return renamed\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _restore_items(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        probe: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        save_path: str,\n"
    "        items: List[Dict[str, Any]],\n"
    "        job_key: str = \"\",\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._restore_items(probe, save_path, items, job_key=job_key) or {})\n"
    "        completed = list(result.get(\"completed_items\") or [])\n"
    "        if completed:\n"
    "            subscribe = self._restore_subscribe_v11224(job_key)\n"
    "            renamed = self._rename_restored_media_v11224(subscribe, save_path, completed)\n"
    "            if renamed:\n"
    "                result[\"renamed_media_v11224\"] = renamed\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaAutoRecoveryV11224Mixin\", \"_prefix_name_v11224\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["content_resilience_v1105"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.5 频道分享追更与文件语义自愈。\n"
    "\n"
    "修复三个会造成真实漏转存的边界：\n"
    "- MoviePilot 当前 total_episode 落后于分享内容时，明确的分享文件集号可以只向上抬高目标范围；\n"

    "- 旧版 processed_entries 没有记录处理时的目标集数，升级后对仍缺集的订阅重新检查一次；\n"
    "  新记录保存 target_total，后续只有目标总集数继续向上增长时才自动重新开放。\n"
    "\n"

    "def _safe_int_v1105(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:\n"

    "def is_auxiliary_media_v1105(path: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "    \"\"\"只识别强语义片头/片尾/预告/样片，避免把正常特别篇误过滤。\"\"\"\n"
    "    normalized = _safe_relative_path(path).replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "    if not normalized:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    parts = [part.strip().lower() for part in normalized.split(\"/\") if part.strip()]\n"
    "    if not parts:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    if any(part in _AUXILIARY_DIR_NAMES for part in parts[:-1]):\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    basename = parts[-1]\n"
    "    stem = re.sub(r\"\\.[a-z0-9]{2,6}$\", \"\", basename, flags=re.I).strip()\n"
    "    stem = re.sub(r\"[\\s._\\-]+\", \"\", stem)\n"
    "    return bool(_AUXILIARY_STEM_RE.fullmatch(stem))\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaContentResilienceV1105Mixin:\n"
