    "                \"cmd\": \"/gywhy\",\n"
    "                \"event\": EventType.PluginAction,\n"
    "                \"desc\": \"查看为什么还没转存：/gywhy 订阅ID或片名\",\n"
    "                \"category\": \"订阅\",\n"
    "                \"data\": {\"action\": \"guangya_explain_existing\"},\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            {\n"
    "                \"cmd\": \"/gystatus\",\n"
    "                \"event\": EventType.PluginAction,\n"
    "                \"desc\": \"查看光鸭转存总体状态\",\n"
    "                \"category\": \"订阅\",\n"
    "                \"data\": {\"action\": \"guangya_route_status\"},\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            {\n"
    "                \"cmd\": \"/gynative\",\n"
    "                \"event\": EventType.PluginAction,\n"
    "                \"desc\": \"切回普通下载：/gynative 订阅ID或片名\",\n"
    "                \"category\": \"订阅\",\n"
    "                \"data\": {\"action\": \"guangya_release_native\"},\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            {\n"
    "                \"cmd\": \"/gyselfcheck\",\n"
    "                \"event\": EventType.PluginAction,\n"
    "                \"desc\": \"检查光鸭登录、频道、分流门禁和待落盘任务\",\n"
    "                \"category\": \"订阅\",\n"
    "                \"data\": {\"action\": \"guangya_selfcheck\"},\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _schedule_pending_route_recovery(self, token: str) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把异常退出留下的路线意图重新写入插件配置，且避免热重载循环。\"\"\"\n"
    "        marker = self.get_data(\"route_recovery_marker\") or {}\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        same_token = str(marker.get(\"token\") or \"\") == str(token or \"\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            age = now - float(marker.get(\"scheduled_at\") or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            age = self._recovery_retry_seconds + 1\n"
    "        if same_token and str(marker.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"scheduled\" and age < self._recovery_retry_seconds:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"route_recovery_marker\", {\n"
    "            \"token\": token,\n"
    "            \"state\": \"scheduled\",\n"
    "            \"scheduled_at\": now,\n"
    "            \"ids\": list(sorted(set(self._selected_subscriptions))),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "\n"
    "        def persist_recovered_route() -> None:\n"
    "            pending = self.get_data(\"route_membership_pending\") or {}\n"
    "            if str(pending.get(\"token\") or \"\") != token:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                self._save_config()\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【崩溃恢复】路线配置重新落盘失败：%s\", err)\n"
    "                self.save_data(\"route_recovery_marker\", {\n"
    "                    \"token\": token,\n"
    "                    \"state\": \"failed\",\n"
    "                    \"scheduled_at\": now,\n"
    "                    \"error\": str(err)[:500],\n"
    "                })\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            latest = self.get_data(\"route_membership_pending\") or {}\n"
    "            if str(latest.get(\"token\") or \"\") == token:\n"
    "                self.save_data(\"route_membership_pending\", {})\n"
    "            self.save_data(\"route_recovery_marker\", {\n"
    "                \"token\": token,\n"
    "                \"state\": \"done\",\n"
    "                \"finished_at\": time.time(),\n"
    "                \"ids\": list(sorted(set(self._selected_subscriptions))),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            self._record_route_health(recovered_route_persisted_at=self._now_text())\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【崩溃恢复】未确认的固定分流路线已重新持久化\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        timer = threading.Timer(max(2.0, float(getattr(self, \"_route_persist_delay\", 1.2)) + 0.8), persist_recovered_route)\n"
    "        timer.daemon = True\n"
    "        timer.start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _guard_one_subscription(self, subscribe: Any, trigger: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"硬阻断原生下载后立即返回，网络刷新和转存全部转入后台。\"\"\"\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        self._record_route_health(\n"
    "            last_guarded_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            last_guarded_id=sid,\n"
    "            last_guarded_name=name,\n"
    "            last_guard_mode=\"async\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【硬分流】%s拦截原生搜索 #%s %s；搜索线程立即返回，光鸭检查转入后台\",\n"
    "            trigger, sid, name,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") not in (\"N\", \"R\"):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"queued\": False,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"固定转存订阅当前非活跃，已阻断原生下载但不执行转存检查\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=trigger)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"handled\": True,\n"
    "            \"queued\": True,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"已阻断 MoviePilot 原生下载，光鸭检查已进入后台队列\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _queue_async_route_check(self, sids: Iterable[int], trigger: str = \"后台检查\") -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"合并短时间内的多个订阅检查，只刷新一次频道，避免搜索线程和频道源互相拖慢。\"\"\"\n"
    "        ids = {\n"
    "            int(value) for value in sids\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if not ids or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "            self._async_route_pending.update(ids)\n"
    "            if self._async_route_worker_running:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            self._async_route_worker_running = True\n"
    "\n"
    "        def worker() -> None:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                # 给一次批量 search 留一个很短的合并窗口，几十个订阅不会各自刷新频道。\n"
    "                time.sleep(self._async_route_debounce)\n"
    "                while self._enabled:\n"
    "                    with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "                        batch = sorted(self._async_route_pending)\n"
    "                        self._async_route_pending.clear()\n"
    "                    if not batch:\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "\n"
    "                    subscriptions = []\n"
    "                    need_refresh = False\n"
    "                    for sid in batch:\n"
    "                        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "                        if not subscribe or not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                        subscriptions.append(subscribe)\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            if not self._cached_matches_for_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                                need_refresh = True\n"
    "                        except Exception:\n"
    "                            need_refresh = True\n"
    "\n"
    "                    if need_refresh and subscriptions:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】%s：%s 个订阅中存在缓存未命中，后台统一刷新一次频道\",\n"
    "                            trigger, len(subscriptions),\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "                        except Exception as err:\n"
    "                            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】频道刷新失败：%s\", err)\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        self._inspect_cache.clear()\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "\n"
    "                    for subscribe in subscriptions:\n"
    "                        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            result = self._try_transfer_subscription(subscribe, refresh_channel=False)\n"
    "                            message = str(result.get(\"message\") or \"检查完成\")\n"
    "                            self._record_route_health(\n"
    "                                last_route_result=message[:500],\n"
    "                                last_route_result_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "                                last_async_check_id=sid,\n"
    "                            )\n"
    "                            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                                \"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】#%s %s：%s\",\n"
    "                                sid, getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"), message,\n"
    "                            )\n"
    "                        except Exception as err:\n"
    "                            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                                \"EXCEPTION\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】#%s %s 异常：%s\",\n"
    "                                sid, getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"), err,\n"
    "                            )\n"
    "                    # 工作期间如果又进来新的订阅，继续下一批；否则退出。\n"
    "                    time.sleep(0.05)\n"
    "            finally:\n"
    "                with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "                    self._async_route_worker_running = False\n"
    "                    restart = bool(self._async_route_pending) and self._enabled\n"
    "                if restart:\n"
    "                    self._queue_async_route_check([], trigger=\"合并补偿\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        threading.Thread(target=worker, name=\"GuangYaAsyncRouteCheck\", daemon=True).start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_any_subscription(self, value: Any) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], List[Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"按 ID 或标题解析 MoviePilot 当前订阅，不要求已在光鸭路线。\"\"\"\n"
    "        query = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        candidates = list(self._list_subscriptions(None) or [])\n"
    "        if not query:\n"
    "            return None, []\n"
    "        if query.isdigit():\n"
    "            exact = [\n"
    "                item for item in candidates\n"
    "                if int(getattr(item, \"id\", 0) or 0) == int(query)\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            return (exact[0] if len(exact) == 1 else None), exact\n"
    "        normalized = _normalize_media_text(query)\n"
    "        matched = [\n"
    "            item for item in candidates\n"
    "            if normalized and normalized in _normalize_media_text(getattr(item, \"name\", \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        exact = [\n"
    "            item for item in matched\n"
    "            if _normalize_media_text(getattr(item, \"name\", \"\")) == normalized\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        if len(exact) == 1:\n"
    "            return exact[0], exact\n"
    "        return (matched[0] if len(matched) == 1 else None), matched\n"
    "\n"
    "    @eventmanager.register(EventType.PluginAction)\n"
    "    def experience_action_event_handler(self, event: Event) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"只处理体验层新增 action；旧 action 继续由 routing_v170 原处理器负责。\"\"\"\n"
    "        event_data = event.event_data or {}\n"
    "        action = event_data.get(\"action\")\n"
    "        if action == \"guangya_takeover_existing\":\n"
    "            self._handle_takeover_existing_command(event_data)\n"
    "        elif action == \"guangya_check_existing\":\n"
    "            self._handle_check_existing_command(event_data)\n"
    "        elif action == \"guangya_explain_existing\":\n"
    "            self._handle_explain_existing_command(event_data)\n"
    "        elif action == \"guangya_selfcheck\":\n"
    "            self._handle_selfcheck_command(event_data)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _command_subscription_or_reply(\n"
    "        self, event_data: Dict[str, Any], *, selected_only: bool = False,\n"
    "    ) -> Optional[Any]:\n"
    "        query = str(event_data.get(\"arg_str\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not query:\n"
    "            self._post_command(event_data, \"缺少订阅\", \"请提供订阅ID或片名。\")\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        if selected_only:\n"
    "            subscribe, matches = self._resolve_selected_subscription(query)\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            subscribe, matches = self._resolve_any_subscription(query)\n"
    "        if subscribe:\n"
    "            return subscribe\n"
    "        if matches:\n"
    "            text = \"\\n\".join(\n"
    "                f\"• #{getattr(item, 'id', 0)} {getattr(item, 'name', '')}\"\n"
    "                for item in matches[:10]\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._post_command(event_data, \"匹配到多个订阅\", text + \"\\n请改用订阅ID。\")\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            self._post_command(event_data, \"未找到订阅\", \"请确认订阅仍存在于 MoviePilot。\")\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_takeover_existing_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        subscribe = self._command_subscription_or_reply(event_data, selected_only=False)\n"
    "        if not subscribe:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "            self._post_command(\n"
    "                event_data, \"已经是光鸭路线\",\n"
    "                f\"#{sid} {getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')}\\n无需重复加入；可发送 /gycheck {sid} 立即检查。\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        self._add_selected_subscription(sid, persist=True)\n"
    "        self._record_route_health(last_takeover_id=sid, last_takeover_at=self._now_text())\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【消息命令】#%s %s 已从普通订阅切到光鸭固定转存\", sid, getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"))\n"
    "        self._post_command(\n"
    "            event_data,\n"
    "            \"✅ 已切到光鸭转存\",\n"
    "            f\"#{sid} {getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')}\\n原生下载已立即阻断；后台正在检查频道资源。\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=\"消息切换路线\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_check_existing_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        subscribe = self._command_subscription_or_reply(event_data, selected_only=True)\n"
    "        if not subscribe:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=\"消息立即检查\")\n"
    "        diagnosis = self._diagnose_subscription(subscribe)\n"
    "        self._post_command(\n"
    "            event_data,\n"
    "            \"🔎 已进入后台检查队列\",\n"
    "            self._format_subscription_diagnosis(diagnosis, include_hint=True),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_explain_existing_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        subscribe = self._command_subscription_or_reply(event_data, selected_only=True)\n"
    "        if not subscribe:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        diagnosis = self._diagnose_subscription(subscribe)\n"
    "        self._post_command(\n"
    "            event_data,\n"
    "            \"为什么还没转存\",\n"
    "            self._format_subscription_diagnosis(diagnosis, include_hint=True),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_selfcheck_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        report = self._build_selfcheck()\n"
    "        self._post_command(event_data, \"光鸭转存自检\", self._format_selfcheck(report))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _diagnose_subscription(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把已有状态机数据翻译成用户能直接理解的“为什么还没转存”。\"\"\"\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        state = str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        done, total, lack = self._subscription_episode_progress(subscribe)\n"
    "        missing = self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            matches = list(self._cached_matches_for_subscription(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            matches = []\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            channel_state = self._channel_state_for_subscription(subscribe)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            channel_state = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        jobs = []\n"
    "        for key, row in (self.get_data(\"transfer_jobs\") or {}).items():\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row_sid = row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or row.get(\"sid\")\n"
    "            if str(row_sid or \"\") == str(sid):\n"
    "                copied = dict(row)\n"
    "                copied[\"_key\"] = key\n"
    "                jobs.append(copied)\n"
    "        pending_status = {\"submitting\", \"submitted\", \"task_confirmed\", \"verifying\"}\n"
    "        pending_jobs = [row for row in jobs if str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") in pending_status]\n"
    "        failed_jobs = [row for row in jobs if str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") == \"failed\"]\n"
    "\n"
    "        index = self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}\n"
    "        index_count = len(index.get(\"items\") or [])\n"
    "        reason = \"等待下一次频道检查\"\n"
    "        severity = \"info\"\n"
    "        if state not in (\"N\", \"R\"):\n"
    "            reason = f\"订阅状态为 {state or '-'}，光鸭路线会保留但不会自动执行\"\n"
    "            severity = \"warning\"\n"
    "        elif pending_jobs:\n"
    "            reason = f\"已有 {len(pending_jobs)} 个转存任务已提交，正在等待光鸭落盘确认\"\n"
    "            severity = \"info\"\n"
    "        elif failed_jobs:\n"
    "            last = failed_jobs[-1]\n"
    "            error = str(last.get(\"error\") or last.get(\"message\") or \"未知错误\")[:160]\n"
    "            reason = f\"最近转存任务失败：{error}\"\n"
    "            severity = \"error\"\n"
    "        elif total and lack <= 0:\n"
    "            if bool(channel_state.get(\"ongoing\")) and not bool(channel_state.get(\"complete\")):\n"
    "                reason = \"目标集已齐，但频道仍标记连载中，正在执行连载保护\"\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                reason = \"目标内容已齐，等待 MoviePilot 完成订阅流程\"\n"
    "            severity = \"success\"\n"
    "        elif matches:\n"
    "            reason = f\"频道已命中 {len(matches)} 条候选，下一步是分享内容检查和增量转存\"\n"
    "            severity = \"info\"\n"
    "        elif index_count <= 0:\n"
    "            reason = \"频道索引为空，后台需要重新抓取频道后才能匹配\"\n"
    "            severity = \"warning\"\n"
    "        elif channel_state.get(\"matched\"):\n"
    "            reason = \"频道能识别到该媒体，但当前缓存候选未通过精确匹配/可转存检查\"\n"
    "            severity = \"warning\"\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            reason = \"当前频道索引没有命中该媒体；不会回退到 MoviePilot 本地下载\"\n"
    "            severity = \"warning\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"id\": sid,\n"
    "            \"name\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"year\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"state\": state,\n"
    "            \"route\": self._is_guangya_route(subscribe),\n"
    "            \"done\": done,\n"
    "            \"total\": total,\n"
    "            \"lack\": lack,\n"
    "            \"missing\": missing[:20],\n"
    "            \"matches\": len(matches),\n"
    "            \"channel_matched\": int(channel_state.get(\"matched\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"channel_ongoing\": bool(channel_state.get(\"ongoing\")),\n"
    "            \"channel_complete\": bool(channel_state.get(\"complete\")),\n"
    "            \"pending_jobs\": len(pending_jobs),\n"
    "            \"failed_jobs\": len(failed_jobs),\n"
    "            \"reason\": reason,\n"
    "            \"severity\": severity,\n"
    "            \"next_check\": f\"约每 {max(1, int(getattr(self, '_refresh_minutes', 10) or 10))} 分钟轮询；也可 /gycheck {sid}\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _format_subscription_diagnosis(row: Dict[str, Any], include_hint: bool = False) -> str:\n"
    "        progress = f\"{row.get('done', 0)}/{row.get('total', 0)}，剩余 {row.get('lack', 0)}\" if row.get(\"total\") else \"电影/总集数未知\"\n"
    "        text = (\n"
    "            f\"#{row.get('id')} {row.get('name')} ({row.get('year') or '-'})\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"当前原因：{row.get('reason')}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"进度：{progress}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"频道命中：{row.get('matches', 0)} 条 · 待落盘：{row.get('pending_jobs', 0)} · 失败任务：{row.get('failed_jobs', 0)}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"下一步：{row.get('next_check')}\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if row.get(\"missing\"):\n"
    "            text += \"\\n缺集：\" + \", \".join(str(value) for value in row[\"missing\"])\n"
    "        if include_hint:\n"
    "            text += f\"\\n操作：/gycheck {row.get('id')} 立即后台检查；/gynative {row.get('id')} 切回普通下载\"\n"
    "        return text\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _build_selfcheck(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"只读取本地运行状态，不主动访问 Telegram；点击自检不会阻塞外部网络。\"\"\"\n"
    "        checks = []\n"
    "\n"
    "        def add(key: str, label: str, ok: bool, detail: str, critical: bool = True) -> None:\n"
    "            checks.append({\n"
    "                \"key\": key,\n"
    "                \"label\": label,\n"
    "                \"ok\": bool(ok),\n"
    "                \"detail\": str(detail or \"\")[:500],\n"
    "                \"critical\": bool(critical),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        add(\"enabled\", \"插件运行\", bool(self._enabled), \"已启用\" if self._enabled else \"插件未启用\")\n"
    "        add(\n"
    "            \"search_guard\",\n"
    "            \"原生搜索硬分流\",\n"
    "            bool(self._is_search_guard_active()),\n"
    "            \"SubscribeChain.search 已接管\" if self._is_search_guard_active() else \"未接管，固定转存可能进入原生搜索\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        match_guard = bool(getattr(SubscribeChain.match, \"_guangya_match_guard\", False))\n"
    "        download_method = getattr(SubscribeChain, \"_SubscribeChain__download_best_version_with_full_pack_first\", None)\n"
    "        download_guard = bool(download_method and getattr(download_method, \"_guangya_download_guard\", False))\n"
    "        add(\"match_guard\", \"RSS/缓存匹配门禁\", match_guard, \"已接管\" if match_guard else \"未接管\")\n"
    "        add(\"download_guard\", \"最终下载断路器\", download_guard, \"已接管\" if download_guard else \"未接管\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            client, runtime = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "            runtime_ok = bool(client)\n"
    "            runtime_detail = \"光鸭云盘助手运行中且可取得客户端\" if runtime_ok else str(runtime or \"光鸭云盘助手未运行或未登录\")\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            runtime_ok = False\n"
    "            runtime_detail = f\"读取光鸭客户端失败：{err}\"\n"
    "        add(\"guangya_runtime\", \"光鸭登录/运行时\", runtime_ok, runtime_detail)\n"
    "\n"
    "        urls = list(self._source_urls() or [])\n"
    "        add(\"channel_sources\", \"频道源配置\", bool(urls), f\"已配置 {len(urls)} 个频道源\" if urls else \"未配置频道源\")\n"
    "        index = self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}\n"
    "        index_items = list(index.get(\"items\") or [])\n"
    "        source_errors = index.get(\"errors\") or []\n"
    "        add(\n"
    "            \"channel_index\",\n"
    "            \"频道本地索引\",\n"
    "            bool(index_items),\n"
    "            f\"{len(index_items)} 条，最近刷新 {index.get('time') or '-'}，最近错误 {len(source_errors)} 个\",\n"
    "            critical=False,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        save_path = str(getattr(self, \"_save_path\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        add(\"save_path\", \"转存目标目录\", bool(save_path), save_path or \"目标目录为空\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        jobs = list((self.get_data(\"transfer_jobs\") or {}).values())\n"
    "        pending = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in jobs\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict) and str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") in {\"submitting\", \"submitted\", \"task_confirmed\", \"verifying\"}\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        failed = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in jobs\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict) and str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") == \"failed\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        add(\"jobs\", \"转存任务状态\", True, f\"待落盘 {pending}，失败 {failed}\", critical=False)\n"
    "\n"
    "        pending_route = self.get_data(\"route_membership_pending\") or {}\n"
    "        add(\n"
    "            \"route_persist\",\n"
    "            \"路线持久化\",\n"
    "            not bool(pending_route.get(\"token\")),\n"
    "            \"已持久化\" if not pending_route.get(\"token\") else \"存在未确认路线写盘意图，已启用崩溃恢复\",\n"
    "            critical=False,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        critical_failed = [item for item in checks if item[\"critical\"] and not item[\"ok\"]]\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"healthy\": not critical_failed,\n"
    "            \"checks\": checks,\n"
    "            \"selected\": len(self._selected_subscriptions),\n"
    "            \"pending_jobs\": pending,\n"
    "            \"failed_jobs\": failed,\n"
    "            \"index_count\": len(index_items),\n"
    "            \"build\": self.build_id,\n"
    "            \"version\": str(getattr(self, \"plugin_version\", \"1.7.0\")),\n"
    "            \"time\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _format_selfcheck(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        rows = [\n"
    "            f\"光鸭转存助手 v{report.get('version')} · build {report.get('build')}\",\n"
    "            f\"总体：{'正常' if report.get('healthy') else '存在关键异常'} · 固定转存 {report.get('selected', 0)} 个\",\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        for item in report.get(\"checks\") or []:\n"
    "            icon = \"✅\" if item.get(\"ok\") else (\"❌\" if item.get(\"critical\") else \"⚠️\")\n"
    "            rows.append(f\"{icon} {item.get('label')}：{item.get('detail')}\")\n"
    "        return \"\\n\".join(rows)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_api(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        apis = list(super().get_api() or [])\n"
    "        existing = {str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\") for item in apis if isinstance(item, dict)}\n"
    "        extras = [\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/selfcheck\", \"endpoint\": self.api_selfcheck, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"光鸭转存助手本地运行状态自检\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/route_guangya\", \"endpoint\": self.api_route_guangya, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"把已有 MoviePilot 订阅加入光鸭固定转存\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/check_route\", \"endpoint\": self.api_check_route, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"后台检查一个光鸭固定转存订阅\"},\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        apis.extend(item for item in extras if item[\"path\"] not in existing)\n"
    "        return apis\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_selfcheck(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        report = self._build_selfcheck()\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"healthy\": report[\"healthy\"],\n"
    "            \"message\": self._format_selfcheck(report),\n"
    "            \"data\": report,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_route_guangya(self, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "        if not sid or not subscribe:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"订阅不存在\"}\n"
    "        changed = self._add_selected_subscription(sid, persist=True)\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=\"页面切换光鸭路线\")\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"message\": (\n"
    "                f\"{getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')} 已切到光鸭固定转存，后台检查已排队\"\n"
    "                if changed else f\"{getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')} 已经是光鸭固定转存路线\"\n"
    "            ),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_check_route(self, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "        if not sid or not subscribe:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"订阅不存在\"}\n"
    "        if not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"该订阅当前不是光鸭固定转存路线\"}\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check([sid], trigger=\"页面后台检查\")\n"
    "        diagnosis = self._diagnose_subscription(subscribe)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"后台检查已排队；当前状态：\" + str(diagnosis.get(\"reason\") or \"等待检查\"),\n"
    "            \"data\": diagnosis,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_form(self):\n"
    "        form, defaults = super().get_form()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            content = form[0].get(\"content\") if form else None\n"
    "            if isinstance(content, list):\n"
    "                content.append({\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                        \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                        \"text\": (\n"
    "                            \"消息快捷操作：/gyroute ID 把已有普通订阅切入光鸭；/gycheck ID 后台立即检查；\"\n"
    "                            \"/gywhy ID 查看未转存原因；/gyselfcheck 检查运行状态。原生订阅搜索只负责触发后台检查，不再等待频道网络。\"\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        pages = list(super().get_page() or [])\n"
    "        report = self._build_selfcheck()\n"
    "        selfcheck = {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mb-3\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardTitle\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": f\"光鸭转存助手 v{report['version']} · build {report['build']}\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": (\n"
    "                        f\"运行自检：{'正常' if report['healthy'] else '存在关键异常'} · \"\n"
    "                        f\"固定转存 {report['selected']} 个 · 频道索引 {report['index_count']} 条 · \"\n"
    "                        f\"待落盘 {report['pending_jobs']} · 失败 {report['failed_jobs']}\"\n"
    "                    ),\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardActions\",\n"
    "                    \"content\": [\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VBtn\",\n"
    "                            \"props\": {\"size\": \"small\", \"variant\": \"outlined\", \"prepend-icon\": \"mdi-stethoscope\"},\n"
    "                            \"text\": \"运行转存自检\",\n"
    "                            \"events\": {\n"
    "                                \"click\": {\n"
    "                                    \"api\": \"plugin/GuangYaTransferAssistant/selfcheck\",\n"
    "                                    \"method\": \"post\",\n"
    "                                    \"params\": {\"apikey\": settings.API_TOKEN},\n"
    "                                }\n"
    "                            },\n"
    "                        }\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "        selected_ids = set(self._selected_subscriptions)\n"
    "        diagnosis_rows = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._list_subscriptions(None):\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid and sid in selected_ids:\n"
    "                diagnosis_rows.append(self._diagnose_subscription(subscribe))\n"
    "        diagnosis_rows = diagnosis_rows[:20]\n"
    "        diagnostic_cards = []\n"
    "        for row in diagnosis_rows:\n"
    "            progress = f\"{row['done']}/{row['total']} · 剩余 {row['lack']}\" if row[\"total\"] else \"电影/总集数未知\"\n"
    "            diagnostic_cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": row[\"severity\"],\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": f\"#{row['id']} {row['name']} ({row['year'] or '-'})\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": (\n"
    "                        f\"{row['reason']} · 进度 {progress} · 频道候选 {row['matches']} · \"\n"
    "                        f\"待落盘 {row['pending_jobs']} · 失败 {row['failed_jobs']} · {row['next_check']}\"\n"
    "                    ),\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        diagnostics = {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"flat\", \"class\": \"mb-3\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardTitle\", \"text\": \"为什么还没转存\"},\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": \"这里直接解释每个固定转存订阅当前停在哪一步；不会因为频道暂时无资源而回退到本地下载。\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                *diagnostic_cards,\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "        if pages:\n"
    "            # 保留 routing_v170 的硬分流健康卡作为第一项，方便运行入口继续补充 RSS/下载断路器状态。\n"
    "            return [pages[0], selfcheck, diagnostics, *pages[1:]]\n"
    "        return [selfcheck, diagnostics]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaExperienceMixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== media_identity_guard_v1111.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["media_identity_guard_v1111"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.11.1 实际资源媒体身份总门禁。\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List\n"
    "\n"
    "from .legacy import _is_video, _subscription_aliases\n"
    "from .media_identity_v1111 import (\n"
    "    assess_media_identity_v1111,\n"
    "    explicit_seasons_v1111,\n"
    "    explicit_years_v1111,\n"
    "    strong_title_match_v1111,\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX_V1111 = \"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaMediaIdentityGuardV1111Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"执行前核验真实 payload；明确冲突硬拒绝，缺字段按多证据置信度处理。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    plugin_version = \"1.11.1\"\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260903-r42\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe: Any) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        aliases = list(_subscription_aliases(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        primary = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if primary and primary not in aliases:\n"
    "            aliases.insert(0, primary)\n"
    "        return aliases\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        raw = str(getattr(subscribe, \"type\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        return \"movie\" in raw.lower() or \"电影\" in raw\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _identity_expected_season_v1111(self, subscribe: Any) -> int:\n"
    "        if self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _provider_candidate_matches(self, subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"搜索阶段只做预筛；最终仍由真实迅雷 JSON / cloud resolve 结果确认。\"\"\"\n"
    "        aliases = self._identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        year = str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        season = self._identity_expected_season_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        is_movie = self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        title_evidence = [row.get(\"search_title\"), row.get(\"name\"), row.get(\"label\")]\n"
    "        if not any(\n"
    "            str(evidence or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            and strong_title_match_v1111(alias, evidence, expected_year=year)\n"
    "            for alias in aliases\n"
    "            for evidence in title_evidence\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        raw_evidence = [*title_evidence, row.get(\"year\")]\n"
    "        years = explicit_years_v1111(raw_evidence, aliases)\n"
    "        if year and years and year not in years:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        seasons = explicit_seasons_v1111(raw_evidence)\n"
    "        if is_movie and seasons:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        if not is_movie and season > 0 and seasons and season not in seasons:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_json_identity_matches_v1123(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        candidate: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        info: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        template: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ):\n"
    "        \"\"\"迅雷最终门禁：实际资源为主，搜索卡片只能补充弱证据，不能覆盖硬冲突。\"\"\"\n"
    "        aliases = self._identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        year = str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        season = self._identity_expected_season_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        search_title = str(candidate.get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        resource_name = str(candidate.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        # GYING 在 panlist 没有 name 时可能把搜索卡片 title 回填到 candidate.name；\n"
    "        # 这种值降级成 discovery 弱证据，不能伪装成真实分享标题。\n"
    "        if resource_name and search_title and resource_name.casefold() == search_title.casefold():\n"
    "            resource_name = \"\"\n"
    "        files = [\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for row in (template.get(\"files\") or [])\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict) and str(row.get(\"path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        assessment = assess_media_identity_v1111(\n"
    "            aliases=aliases,\n"
    "            expected_year=year,\n"
    "            expected_season=season,\n"
    "            is_movie=self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe),\n"
    "            primary_evidences=[str(info.get(\"title\") or \"\").strip(), resource_name],\n"
    "            file_evidences=files[:300],\n"
    "            discovery_evidences=[search_title, candidate.get(\"label\")],\n"
    "            threshold=50,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not assessment.get(\"ok\"):\n"
    "            return False, f\"迅雷实际资源身份拒绝：{assessment.get('reason') or '置信度不足'}\"\n"
    "        return True, f\"迅雷媒体身份通过：score={assessment.get('score', 0)}；{assessment.get('reason') or ''}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Magnet/ED2K resolve 后用真实 btResInfo/subfiles 做最终身份确认。\"\"\"\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        data = result.get(\"resolve_data\") if isinstance(result.get(\"resolve_data\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        bt_info = data.get(\"btResInfo\") if isinstance(data.get(\"btResInfo\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        files: List[str] = []\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") if isinstance(bt_info.get(\"subfiles\"), list) else []\n"
    "        for row in subfiles[:500]:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            value = str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value:\n"
    "                files.append(value)\n"
    "        season = self._identity_expected_season_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "        assessment = assess_media_identity_v1111(\n"
    "            aliases=self._identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe),\n"
    "            expected_year=getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None),\n"
    "            expected_season=season,\n"
    "            is_movie=self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe),\n"
    "            primary_evidences=[\n"
    "                str(result.get(\"resolved_name\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                str(bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "            file_evidences=files,\n"
    "            discovery_evidences=[\n"
    "                source.get(\"search_title\"), source.get(\"title\"), source.get(\"name\"), source.get(\"label\")\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "            threshold=50,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not assessment.get(\"ok\"):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX_V1111}媒体身份门禁：{assessment.get('reason') or '置信度不足'}\"\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        result[\"identity_score_v1111\"] = int(assessment.get(\"score\") or 0)\n"
    "        result[\"identity_reason_v1111\"] = str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"\")\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _plan_incremental_files(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        probe: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        assets: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any = None,\n"
    "        target_path: str = \"\",\n"
    "        stats: Dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n"
    "    ):\n"
    "        \"\"\"直接分享先处理硬冲突；缺少年份/季号不会因为信息不足直接拒绝。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "            paths = [\n"
    "                str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                for row in (probe.get(\"files\") or [])\n"
    "                if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "                and _is_video(str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            if paths:\n"
    "                aliases = self._identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "                year = str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                years = explicit_years_v1111(paths, aliases)\n"
    "                season = self._identity_expected_season_v1111(subscribe)\n"
    "                seasons = explicit_seasons_v1111(paths)\n"
    "                if year and years and year not in years:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【媒体身份】直接分享年份冲突，拒绝规划：期望=%s 实际=%s\",\n"
    "                        year,\n"
    "                        sorted(years),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return []\n"
    "                if self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe) and seasons:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【媒体身份】电影直接分享出现季号 %s，拒绝规划\",\n"
    "                        sorted(seasons),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return []\n"
    "                if not self._identity_is_movie_v1111(subscribe) and season > 0 and seasons and season not in seasons:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【媒体身份】直接分享季号冲突，期望=S%02d 实际=%s，拒绝规划\",\n"
    "                        season,\n"
    "                        sorted(seasons),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return []\n"
    "        return super()._plan_incremental_files(\n"
    "            probe,\n"
    "            assets,\n"
    "            subscribe=subscribe,\n"
    "            target_path=target_path,\n"
    "            stats=stats,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaMediaIdentityGuardV1111Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== media_match_v11219.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["media_match_v11219"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.19 高质量媒体匹配与强 claim 语义。\n"
    "\n"
    "这一层只做两件事：\n"
    "1. 电影：真正执行前必须从真实分享/resolve 结果中得到可验证的视频与作品标题证据；\n"
    "2. 剧集/动漫：把“想补哪些集”与“真实解析后确认哪些集”彻底分开，只有已经\n"
    "   resolve 并准备/已经提交给光鸭的物理集，才形成跨来源强 claim。\n"
    "\n"
    "兼容原则：\n"
    "- target_episodes 继续保留，作为旧 UI / 持久化兼容字段，但只表示 requested intent；\n"
    "- requested_episodes 表示本次希望补的集；\n"
    "- candidate_episodes 仅表示搜索标题/帖子中可高置信推断出的候选集；\n"
    "- resolved_episodes 表示真实 payload 中确认的集；\n"
    "- transfer_episodes 表示最终允许提交给光鸭的物理集；\n"
    "- 老版本已经存在 taskId 但没有新字段时，submitted/queued/waiting/completed 仍可回退\n"
    "  target_episodes 形成 claim，避免升级后把既有远端任务误当成不存在。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Set\n"
    "\n"
    "from .episode_resolver_v190 import AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE, reliable_episode_set, resolve_episode\n"
    "from .legacy import _is_video\n"
    "from .media_identity_v1111 import assess_media_identity_v1111\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_STRONG_SOURCE_STATES_V11219 = {\"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\", \"completed\"}\n"
    "_ACTIVE_SOURCE_STATES_V11219 = {\n"
    "    \"new\", \"retry\", \"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\", \"completed\"\n"
    "}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _positive_episode_set_v11219(values: Iterable[Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "    result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "    for raw in values or []:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if value > 0:\n"
    "            result.add(value)\n"
    "    return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def source_claim_episodes_v11219(source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"只返回已经具备强执行事实的集数；new/retry 的意图绝不占坑。\"\"\"\n"
    "    row = dict(source or {})\n"
    "    state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\").strip().lower()\n"
    "    verified = _positive_episode_set_v11219(row.get(\"transfer_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "    if not verified:\n"
    "        verified = _positive_episode_set_v11219(row.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "    if state in _STRONG_SOURCE_STATES_V11219:\n"
    "        if verified:\n"
    "            return verified\n"
    "        # 升级兼容：旧版本 task 已经真实存在但没有 resolved/transfer 字段。\n"
    "        if str(row.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            return _positive_episode_set_v11219(row.get(\"target_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        return set()\n"
    "\n"
    "    # dispatching 只有已经通过真实 payload 验证时才允许成为强 claim。\n"
    "    if state == \"dispatching\" and bool(row.get(\"media_match_verified_v11219\")):\n"
    "        return verified\n"
    "    return set()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def movie_actual_match_v11219(\n"
    "    *,\n"
    "    aliases: Iterable[str],\n"
    "    expected_year: Any,\n"
    "    primary_evidences: Iterable[Any],\n"
    "    file_evidences: Iterable[Any],\n"
    ") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"电影最终身份只看真实 payload，不允许 discovery 单独把资源救回来。\"\"\"\n"
    "    alias_rows = [str(value or \"\").strip() for value in aliases or [] if str(value or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "    primary = [str(value or \"\").strip() for value in primary_evidences or [] if str(value or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "    files = [str(value or \"\").strip() for value in file_evidences or [] if str(value or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "    assessment = assess_media_identity_v1111(\n"
    "        aliases=alias_rows,\n"
    "        expected_year=expected_year,\n"
    "        expected_season=0,\n"
    "        is_movie=True,\n"
    "        primary_evidences=primary,\n"
    "        file_evidences=files,\n"
    "        discovery_evidences=(),\n"
    "        threshold=45,\n"
    "    )\n"
    "    actual_title_match = bool(assessment.get(\"primary_match\") or assessment.get(\"file_match\"))\n"
    "    if not bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")) or not actual_title_match:\n"
    "        reason = str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"真实电影身份不足\")\n"
    "        if bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")) and not actual_title_match:\n"
    "            reason = \"真实 payload 没有命中任何 MoviePilot/TMDB 官方标题别名\"\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            **dict(assessment),\n"
    "            \"ok\": False,\n"
    "            \"actual_title_match\": actual_title_match,\n"
    "            \"reason\": reason,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        **dict(assessment),\n"
    "        \"ok\": True,\n"
    "        \"actual_title_match\": True,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaMediaMatchV11219Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"电影作品身份 + 剧集物理集两条最终匹配链的共同收口。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    match_schema_v11219 = 1\n"
    "    _completed_claim_grace_seconds_v11219 = 15 * 60\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _candidate_episode_set_v11219(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        label: str = \"\",\n"
    "        episode_hint: str = \"\",\n"
    "    ) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        if subscribe is None or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        paths = [value for value in (str(label or \"\").strip(), str(episode_hint or \"\").strip()) if value]\n"
    "        result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        threshold = float(getattr(self, \"_episode_auto_confidence\", AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE) or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "        for value in paths:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                parsed = resolve_episode(\n"
    "                    value,\n"
    "                    package_paths=paths,\n"
    "                    season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None),\n"
    "                    episode_hint=str(episode_hint or \"\"),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                result.update(reliable_episode_set(parsed, threshold))\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _upsert_source(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any):\n"
    "        \"\"\"保存 requested/candidate intent；不把它们提升成已验证 claim。\"\"\"\n"
    "        row = dict(super()._upsert_source(*args, **kwargs) or {})\n"
    "        if not row:\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "\n"
    "        subscribe_id = int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or (args[0] if args else kwargs.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(subscribe_id) if subscribe_id else None\n"
    "        is_movie = bool(subscribe is not None and self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe))\n"
    "        requested = set()\n"
    "        if not is_movie:\n"
    "            requested = _positive_episode_set_v11219(\n"
    "                kwargs.get(\"target_episodes\")\n"
    "                if \"target_episodes\" in kwargs\n"
    "                else row.get(\"requested_episodes\") or row.get(\"target_episodes\") or []\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        candidate = self._candidate_episode_set_v11219(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            label=str(kwargs.get(\"label\") or row.get(\"label\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            episode_hint=str(kwargs.get(\"episode_hint\") or row.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\"),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        fields: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "            \"match_schema_v11219\": int(self.match_schema_v11219),\n"
    "            \"media_scope_v11219\": \"movie\" if is_movie else \"series\",\n"
    "            \"requested_episodes\": sorted(requested),\n"
    "            \"candidate_episodes\": sorted(candidate),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if \"media_match_verified_v11219\" not in row:\n"
    "            fields[\"media_match_verified_v11219\"] = False\n"
    "        updated = self._update_source(str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\"), **fields)\n"
    "        return dict(updated or row)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _active_source_claims(self, subscribe_id: int) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        return self._effective_source_claims_v11222(subscribe_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _effective_source_claims_v11222(self, subscribe_id: int, current_source_id: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Planning and final payload checks must use the same claim lifetime.\"\"\"\n"
    "        claims: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "        missing_now: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        if subscribe is not None and not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            for raw in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if value > 0:\n"
    "                    missing_now.add(value)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            items = (self._source_store().get(\"items\") or {}).values()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return claims\n"
    "        for row in items:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict) or int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if current_source_id and str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\") == str(current_source_id):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if not bool(row.get(\"enabled\", True)):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row_claims = source_claim_episodes_v11219(row)\n"
    "            if not row_claims:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "            overlap = row_claims.intersection(missing_now)\n"
    "            if state == \"completed\" and overlap:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    completed_ts = float(row.get(\"completed_ts\") or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    completed_ts = 0.0\n"
    "                grace = max(60, int(getattr(self, \"_completed_claim_grace_seconds_v11219\", 15 * 60) or 15 * 60))\n"
    "                if completed_ts <= 0:\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【来源Claim】#%s 释放无 completed_ts 的 completed 占坑：source=%s episodes=%s\",\n"
    "                            sid,\n"
    "                            str(row.get(\"id\") or \"-\")[:60],\n"
    "                            sorted(overlap),\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "                    row_claims = row_claims - overlap\n"
    "                    if not row_claims:\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "                if completed_ts > 0 and time.time() - completed_ts >= grace:\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【来源Claim】#%s 释放过期 completed 占坑：source=%s episodes=%s age=%ss\",\n"
    "                            sid,\n"
    "                            str(row.get(\"id\") or \"-\")[:60],\n"
    "                            sorted(overlap),\n"
    "                            int(time.time() - completed_ts),\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "                    row_claims = row_claims - overlap\n"
    "                    if not row_claims:\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "            claims.update(row_claims)\n"
    "        return claims\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _other_source_claims_v11214(self, subscribe_id: int, current_source_id: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        return self._effective_source_claims_v11222(subscribe_id, current_source_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _source_episode_targets_v1124(source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"EpisodeFence 只对真实提交集做取消/裁剪；未验证 intent 不再形成跨来源终止事实。\"\"\"\n"
    "        return source_claim_episodes_v11219(source)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _movie_actual_gate_v11219(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        primary: Iterable[Any],\n"
    "        files: Iterable[Any],\n"
    "        origin: str,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        aliases_fn = getattr(self, \"_identity_aliases_v1111\", None)\n"
    "        aliases = list(aliases_fn(subscribe) if callable(aliases_fn) else [str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")])\n"
    "        assessment = movie_actual_match_v11219(\n"
    "            aliases=aliases,\n"
    "            expected_year=getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None),\n"
    "            primary_evidences=primary,\n"
    "            file_evidences=files,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【电影最终匹配v1.12.19】#%s %s origin=%s 拒绝：%s\",\n"
    "                int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "                str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                str(origin or \"-\")[:40],\n"
    "                str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"\")[:320],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return assessment\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _plan_incremental_files(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        probe: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        assets: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any = None,\n"
    "        target_path: str = \"\",\n"
    "        stats: Dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n"
    "    ):\n"
    "        planned = list(super()._plan_incremental_files(\n"
    "            probe,\n"
    "            assets,\n"
    "            subscribe=subscribe,\n"
    "            target_path=target_path,\n"
    "            stats=stats,\n"
    "        ) or [])\n"
    "        if not planned or subscribe is None or not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return planned\n"
    "\n"
    "        video_paths = [\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for row in (probe.get(\"files\") or [])\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "            and _is_video(str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        if not video_paths:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        roots_fn = getattr(self, \"_direct_share_primary_roots_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        primary: List[str] = []\n"
    "        if callable(roots_fn):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                primary = list(roots_fn(video_paths, getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None)) or [])\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                primary = []\n"
    "        assessment = self._movie_actual_gate_v11219(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            primary=primary,\n"
    "            files=video_paths,\n"
    "            origin=\"guangya_share\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")):\n"
    "            if stats is not None:\n"
    "                stats[\"movie_actual_reject_v11219\"] = 1\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        return planned\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        resolve_data = result.get(\"resolve_data\") if isinstance(result.get(\"resolve_data\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") if isinstance(resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") if isinstance(bt_info.get(\"subfiles\"), list) else []\n"
    "\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            video_files = []\n"
    "            for row in subfiles:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                name = str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if name and _is_video(name):\n"
    "                    video_files.append(name)\n"
    "            resolved_name = str(result.get(\"resolved_name\") or bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not video_files and resolved_name and _is_video(resolved_name):\n"
    "                video_files.append(resolved_name)\n"
    "            if not video_files:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:电影真实 payload 未发现可验证的视频文件\")\n"
    "\n"
    "            assessment = self._movie_actual_gate_v11219(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                primary=[resolved_name, str(bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()],\n"
    "                files=video_files,\n"
    "                origin=str(source.get(\"type\") or \"offline\"),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if not bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    \"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:电影真实资源身份拒绝：\"\n"
    "                    + str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"置信度不足\")[:320]\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            if source_id:\n"
    "                self._update_source(\n"
    "                    source_id,\n"
    "                    media_match_verified_v11219=True,\n"
    "                    media_match_score_v11219=int(assessment.get(\"score\") or 0),\n"
    "                    media_match_reason_v11219=str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"\")[:320],\n"
    "                    requested_episodes=[],\n"
    "                    candidate_episodes=[],\n"
    "                    resolved_episodes=[],\n"
    "                    transfer_episodes=[],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            result[\"media_match_verified_v11219\"] = True\n"
    "            result[\"media_match_score_v11219\"] = int(assessment.get(\"score\") or 0)\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        physical = _positive_episode_set_v11219(result.get(\"physical_episodes_v11214\") or [])\n"
    "        latest: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        if source_id:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                latest = dict((self._source_store().get(\"items\") or {}).get(source_id) or {})\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                latest = {}\n"
    "        if not physical:\n"
    "            physical = _positive_episode_set_v11219(latest.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        if not physical:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:真实资源已解析，但无法确认任何可转存剧集\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        allowed_fn = getattr(self, \"_authoritative_missing_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(allowed_fn):\n"
    "            allowed = set(allowed_fn(subscribe, current_source_id=source_id) or set())\n"
    "            if not physical.issubset(allowed):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    \"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:最终物理集超出 MoviePilot 当前真实缺集：\"\n"
    "                    f\"physical={sorted(physical)} allowed={sorted(allowed)}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "\n"
    "        requested = _positive_episode_set_v11219(\n"
    "            latest.get(\"requested_episodes\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"requested_episodes\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"target_episodes\")\n"
    "            or []\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        candidate = _positive_episode_set_v11219(\n"
    "            latest.get(\"candidate_episodes\") or source.get(\"candidate_episodes\") or []\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if source_id:\n"
    "            self._update_source(\n"
    "                source_id,\n"
    "                media_match_verified_v11219=True,\n"
    "                media_match_score_v11219=int(result.get(\"identity_score_v1111\") or 0),\n"
    "                media_match_reason_v11219=str(result.get(\"identity_reason_v1111\") or \"真实媒体身份与物理集均已通过\")[:320],\n"
    "                requested_episodes=sorted(requested),\n"
    "                candidate_episodes=sorted(candidate),\n"
    "                resolved_episodes=sorted(physical),\n"
    "                transfer_episodes=sorted(physical),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        result[\"transfer_episodes_v11219\"] = sorted(physical)\n"
    "        result[\"media_match_verified_v11219\"] = True\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_json_identity_matches_v1123(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        candidate: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        info: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        template: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"迅雷电影最终执行前按真实分享标题与视频路径再次验真。\"\"\"\n"
    "        accepted, reason = super()._xunlei_json_identity_matches_v1123(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            candidate,\n"
    "            info,\n"
    "            template,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not accepted or not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        search_title = str(candidate.get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        resource_name = str(candidate.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        # panlist 缺 name 时会把 search_title 回填到 candidate.name；它仍只是 discovery。\n"
    "        if resource_name and search_title and resource_name.casefold() == search_title.casefold():\n"
    "            resource_name = \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        video_files = [\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for row in (template.get(\"files\") or [])\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "            and _is_video(str(row.get(\"path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        if not video_files:\n"
    "            return False, \"迅雷电影真实 payload 未发现可验证的视频文件\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        aliases_fn = getattr(self, \"_identity_aliases_v1111\", None)\n"
    "        aliases = list(\n"
    "            aliases_fn(subscribe)\n"
    "            if callable(aliases_fn)\n"
    "            else [str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")]\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        primary = [\n"
    "            str(info.get(\"title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            resource_name,\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        assessment = movie_actual_match_v11219(\n"
    "            aliases=aliases,\n"
    "            expected_year=getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None),\n"
    "            primary_evidences=primary,\n"
    "            file_evidences=video_files,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if bool(assessment.get(\"ok\")):\n"
    "            return True, (\n"
    "                \"迅雷电影真实 payload 通过：\"\n"
    "                + str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or f\"score={assessment.get('score', 0)}\")[:300]\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 保留 v1.12.16 已证明安全的严格双语真实资源桥；不做其它模糊救回。\n"
    "        bridge = getattr(self, \"_bilingual_bridge_v11216\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(bridge):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                rescued, bridge_reason = bridge(subscribe, candidate, info, template)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                rescued, bridge_reason = False, \"\"\n"
    "            if rescued:\n"
    "                return True, f\"迅雷电影真实 payload 双语闭环通过：{str(bridge_reason or '')[:300]}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        return False, (\n"
    "            \"迅雷电影真实 payload 身份拒绝：\"\n"
    "            + str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"实际标题未命中 MoviePilot/TMDB 官方别名\")[:320]\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaMediaMatchV11219Mixin\",\n"
    "    \"movie_actual_match_v11219\",\n"
    "    \"source_claim_episodes_v11219\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["multisource_v180"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.8.0 多来源与原生云添加层。\n"
    "\n"
    "核心原则：\n"
    "- Magnet 与 ED2K 都是“订阅来源”，不是 MoviePilot 下载器任务；\n"

    "- resolve_res -> create_task -> list_task 构成可恢复状态机；\n"
    "- 外部来源一旦绑定订阅，继续沿用已有固定分流门禁，避免原生下载重复获取；\n"
    "- “观影”按 MoviePilot 订阅模式接入：已有订阅可直接绑定 Magnet/ED2K 来源。\n"

    "class GuangYaMultiSourceMixin(GuangYaSourceStoreMixin):\n"
    "    \"\"\"Magnet/ED2K -> 光鸭原生云添加，并提供 MoviePilot 观影订阅接入口。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260901-r1\"\n"
    "    _offline_batch_limit = 20\n"
    "    # 服务端任务已经 completed、但 list_task 未返回可验证视频文件名时，\n"
    "    # 最多保留一段“远端完成待核验”窗口。窗口结束后必须解除 episode claim，\n"
    "    # 否则一个信息不完整的回执会永久阻塞后续候选。\n"
    "    _offline_remote_verify_grace_seconds = 20 * 60\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._offline_lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._offline_worker_lock = threading.Lock()\n"
    "        self._offline_worker_ids: set[str] = set()\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _claim_source_dispatch_slot(self, source_id: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        source_id = str(source_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not source_id:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_offline_worker_lock\", None)\n"
    "        if lock is None:\n"
    "            lock = threading.Lock()\n"
    "            self._offline_worker_lock = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            workers = getattr(self, \"_offline_worker_ids\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(workers, set):\n"
    "                workers = set()\n"
    "                self._offline_worker_ids = workers\n"
    "            if source_id in workers:\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "            workers.add(source_id)\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _release_source_dispatch_slot(self, source_id: str) -> None:\n"
    "        source_id = str(source_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not source_id:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_offline_worker_lock\", None)\n"
    "        workers = getattr(self, \"_offline_worker_ids\", None)\n"
    "        if lock is None or not isinstance(workers, set):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            workers.discard(source_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 光鸭原生云添加 API\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _offline_api_success(response: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        if not isinstance(response, dict):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        if response.get(\"error\"):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        msg = str(response.get(\"msg\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        code = response.get(\"code\")\n"
    "        if msg == \"success\":\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        if msg:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return code in (None, 0, \"0\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _offline_api_error(response: Any, fallback: str) -> str:\n"
    "        if not isinstance(response, dict):\n"
    "            return fallback\n"
    "        return str(\n"
    "            response.get(\"error_description\")\n"
    "            or response.get(\"error\")\n"
    "            or response.get(\"msg\")\n"
    "            or response.get(\"message\")\n"
    "            or fallback\n"
    "        )[:500]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _offline_request(self, endpoint: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"调用光鸭原生 cloudcollection；优先使用客户端公开方法，兼容旧客户端。\"\"\"\n"
    "        client, _ = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not client:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"光鸭云盘助手未运行或未登录\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        endpoint = str(endpoint or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        method_map = {\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v1/resolve_res\": \"cloudcollection_resolve_res\",\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v1/create_task\": \"cloudcollection_task_create\",\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v1/list_task\": \"cloudcollection_task_list\",\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v2/retry_task\": \"cloudcollection_task_retry\",\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v2/delete_task\": \"cloudcollection_task_delete\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        method_name = method_map.get(endpoint)\n"
    "        method = getattr(client, method_name, None) if method_name else None\n"
    "        if callable(method):\n"
    "            result = method(payload)\n"
    "            return result if isinstance(result, dict) else {\"msg\": \"error\", \"error\": str(result)}\n"
    "\n"
    "        request = getattr(client, \"_request\", None)\n"
    "        base_url = str(getattr(client, \"API_BASE_URL\", \"https://api.guangyapan.com\") or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if not callable(request):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"当前光鸭客户端缺少 cloudcollection 请求能力\")\n"
    "        result = request(\n"
    "            method=\"POST\",\n"
    "            url=f\"{base_url}{endpoint}\",\n"
    "            data=dict(payload or {}),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return result if isinstance(result, dict) else {\"msg\": \"error\", \"error\": str(result)}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _offline_target_parent(self, subscribe: Any) -> Tuple[str, str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"复用光鸭存储插件创建/解析目标目录，返回 path 与 parentId。\"\"\"\n"
    "        _, api = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not api:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"光鸭云盘助手存储运行时不可用\")\n"
    "        target_path = str(self._target_path(subscribe) or \"/\")\n"
    "        folder = api.get_folder(Path(target_path))\n"
    "        if not folder:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"无法创建或定位光鸭目标目录：{target_path}\")\n"
    "        parent_id = \"\" if target_path.rstrip(\"/\") in (\"\", \"/\") else str(folder.fileid or \"\")\n"
    "        if target_path.rstrip(\"/\") not in (\"\", \"/\") and not parent_id:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"光鸭目标目录缺少 fileId：{target_path}\")\n"
    "        return target_path, parent_id\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _offline_remote_entry_meta(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, str, int, bool]:\n"
    "        file_id = str(row.get(\"fileId\") or row.get(\"fileid\") or row.get(\"file_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        name = str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or row.get(\"file_name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            size = max(0, int(row.get(\"fileSize\") or row.get(\"size\") or row.get(\"file_size\") or 0))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            size = 0\n"
    "        is_dir = (\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"resType\") or \"\").strip() == \"2\"\n"
    "            or str(row.get(\"type\") or row.get(\"kind\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "            in {\"dir\", \"folder\", \"drive#folder\", \"directory\"}\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return file_id, name, size, is_dir\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _offline_parent_rows_readback(self, parent_id: str) -> Tuple[bool, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"只读枚举光鸭目标目录；失败与空目录必须严格区分。\"\"\"\n"
    "        client, _ = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        getter = getattr(client, \"get_file_list\", None) if client else None\n"
    "        if not callable(getter):\n"
    "            return False, []\n"
    "\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for page in range(3):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    body = getter(\n"
    "                        parent_id=str(parent_id or \"\"),\n"
    "                        page_size=100,\n"
    "                        order_by=3,\n"
    "                        sort_type=1,\n"
    "                        file_types=[],\n"
    "                        page=page,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                except TypeError:\n"
    "                    body = getter(parent_id=str(parent_id or \"\"), page_size=100, page=page)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"DEBUG\",\n"
    "                    \"【真实落盘核验】目标目录读取失败 parent=%s page=%s error=%s\",\n"
    "                    str(parent_id or \"\")[:80],\n"
    "                    page,\n"
    "                    str(err)[:260],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return False, []\n"
    "\n"
    "            if not isinstance(body, dict):\n"
    "                return False, []\n"
    "            code = body.get(\"code\")\n"
    "            msg = str(body.get(\"msg\") or body.get(\"message\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "            ok = (\n"
    "                not body.get(\"error\")\n"
    "                and (code in (None, 0, \"0\", 200, \"200\") or msg in {\"success\", \"ok\"})\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if not ok:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"DEBUG\",\n"
    "                    \"【真实落盘核验】目标目录接口返回失败 parent=%s page=%s code=%s msg=%s\",\n"
    "                    str(parent_id or \"\")[:80],\n"
    "                    page,\n"
    "                    str(code)[:40],\n"
    "                    msg[:160],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return False, []\n"
    "\n"
    "            data = body.get(\"data\") or {}\n"
    "            if not isinstance(data, dict):\n"
    "                return False, []\n"
    "            listing = data.get(\"list\") or data.get(\"items\") or []\n"
    "            page_rows = [dict(row) for row in listing if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "            rows.extend(page_rows)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                total = max(0, int(data.get(\"total\") or 0))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                total = 0\n"
    "            if not page_rows or len(page_rows) < 100 or (total and len(rows) >= total):\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        return True, rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _offline_landing_snapshot(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        parent_id: str,\n"
    "        manifest: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]],\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        max_depth: int = 2,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"对本次计划中的正片做有界只读快照，用于 completed 后证明“新文件真实出现”.\"\"\"\n"
    "        expected: Dict[str, int] = {}\n"
    "        for raw in manifest or []:\n"
    "            row = dict(raw or {})\n"
    "            name = str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\") != \"video\" and not _is_video(name):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            base = name.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1].strip().casefold()\n"
    "            if not base:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                size = max(0, int(row.get(\"size\") or 0))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                size = 0\n"
    "            expected[base] = max(expected.get(base, 0), size)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not expected:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"ready\": False,\n"
    "                \"reason\": \"no_expected_video_manifest\",\n"
    "                \"file_ids\": [],\n"
